# Hezekai Wiki

访客入口由站点首页组件呈现。下方是 OKF 知识目录（与侧栏同源）。

The visitor entry is rendered by the site home component. The OKF knowledge directory follows (same catalog as the sidebar).


## 站内入口 / Site entry points

- [概览 / Overview](./overview.md) — 内容与页面组织。 / what the wiki holds and how pages are arranged.
- [关于与维护 / About and maintenance](./guides/about-and-maintenance.md) — 方法、双语约定与维护命令。 / method, bilingual convention, and maintainer commands.
- [编译页面 / Compiled pages](./concepts/compiled-pages.md) — Markdown 如何成为发布页。 / how a Markdown file becomes a published page.
- [添加页面 / Adding a page](./guides/adding-a-page.md) — 新页面放哪里。 / where a new page goes.
- [X 简报 / X digests](./sources/x-digest/index.md) — 每日精选归档。 / curated daily briefs archive.
- [日志 / Log](./log.md) — 入库时间线。 / filing timeline.
- [主题 / Topics](./topics/index.md) · [文章 / Articles](./sources/articles/index.md)

- [Skipper：JVM 耐久工作流执行库，非集群 / Skipper: Durable Workflow Execution for the JVM — a Library, Not a Cluster](./sources/articles/skipper-durable-workflow-execution-for-the-jvm-a-library-not-a-c.md) — Skipper 是 Airbnb 开源的轻量级 JVM 工作流引擎库，让业务流程以普通 Kotlin/Java 代码编写，并保证在崩溃、部署和下游故障下仍能运行到终止状态。 / Skipper is Airbnb's open-source lightweight JVM workflow engine library that lets you write business processes as ordinary Kotlin or Java code and guarantees they run to completion despite crashes, deploys, and flaky downstreams.

- [OpenAI 发售 200 美元 Codex 版 Chromatic 掌机，一句话用 AI 做 Game Boy 游戏 / OpenAI Sells $200 Codex Edition Chromatic Handheld, Make Game Boy Games with One Sentence via AI](./sources/articles/openai-sells-200-codex-edition-chromatic-handheld-make-game-boy.md) — OpenAI 与 ModRetro 联合推出 Codex 版 Chromatic 掌机，售价 200 美元，用户通过 Codex 插件用自然语言描述游戏构思，生成可在模拟器或真机运行的 Game Boy ROM。 / OpenAI and ModRetro launch the $200 Codex edition Chromatic handheld, letting users describe game ideas in natural language via the Codex plugin to generate Game Boy ROMs runnable on emulators or the device.

- [为什么要向 AI 代理讲清问题而非方案：一个提升面谈质量的技巧 / Why describe the problem to an AI agent rather than prescribe a solution: one tip for better grilling](./sources/articles/why-describe-the-problem-to-an-ai-agent-rather-than-prescribe-a.md) — 帖文称，向 AI 代理说明问题背景（“为什么”）而非直接给出解决方案（“什么”），能获得更深入、更具创造性的对话。 / The post says explaining the problem context (“why”) to an AI agent instead of prescribing a solution (“what”) leads to deeper, more creative conversations.

- [Simon · PR 折叠：隐藏 AI 生成的 PR 描述 / Simon · PR Fold: Hiding AI-Generated PR Descriptions](./sources/articles/simon-pr-fold-hiding-ai-generated-pr-descriptions.md) — Simon Willison 建议在 GitHub 中用 `<details><summary>` 折叠 AI 生成的 PR 描述，以降低阅读 AI 生成 PR 滥竽充数内容的疲劳感。 / Simon Willison suggests collapsing AI-generated GitHub PR descriptions behind `<details><summary>` to reduce fatigue from reading PR slop.

- [Muse Ember 成本对比：每任务成本约为 Claude Haiku 5.5 三分之一 / Muse Ember cost comparison: roughly one-third the cost per task of Claude Haiku 5.5](./sources/articles/muse-ember-cost-comparison-roughly-one-third-the-cost-per-task-o.md) — 帖文称 Meta 的 Muse Ember 在人工智能分析智能指数上与 Claude Haiku 5.5 性能相当，但每任务成本仅约三分之一，且在 GPT-6 Luna 的所有推理设置中得分更高。 / The post claims Meta's Muse Ember matches Claude Haiku 5.5 on the Artificial Analysis Intelligence Index at about one-third the cost per task, and scores higher than GPT-6 Luna across all Luna reasoning settings.

- [把 Grok Bot 从 100 刀降到 30 刀：重新审视个人 Agent 与数据主权 / Downgrading Grok Bot from $100 to $30: Reassessing Personal Agents and Data Sovereignty](./sources/articles/downgrading-grok-bot-from-100-to-30-reassessing-personal-agents.md) — 帖文称因模型路由不透明、额度消耗大且体验下降，作者将 Grok Bot 订阅从 100 美元降至 30 美元，并考虑迁移至 OpenBot 或 OpenMausBot 等开源替代方案。 / The post says that due to opaque model routing, high quota consumption, and declining experience, the author downgraded the Grok Bot subscription from $100 to $30 and is considering open-source alternatives such as OpenBot and OpenMausBot.

- [DeepSeek Harness 定位 Agent 时代操作系统 / DeepSeek Harness Positioned as Agent Era Operating System](./sources/articles/deepseek-harness-positioned-as-agent-era-operating-system.md) — 宝玉在 X 帖文中称，DeepSeek 希望 DeepSeek Harness 作为 Agent 时代的操作系统，并讨论内置功能与第三方插件的关系。 / Bao Yu says in an X post that DeepSeek hopes DeepSeek Harness serves as the Agent era's operating system, discussing built-in features and third-party plugins.

- [铁锤人：billion-context 让模型自主压缩编程 Agent 上下文 / Tie Churen: billion-context Lets Models Self-Compress Coding Agent Context](./sources/articles/tie-churen-billion-context-lets-models-self-compress-coding-agen.md) — 帖文称，billion-context 是架在编程 Agent 与模型 API 之间的开源上下文压缩代理，由模型自主调用工具管理长会话上下文。 / The post says billion-context is an open-source context-compression proxy between coding agents and model APIs, letting the model self-manage long-session context via tools.

- [CapWise（灵录）录屏工具基本可用，AI 新增 6.43 万行代码 / CapWise (Linglu) screen recorder basically usable; AI added 64,300 lines of code](./sources/articles/capwise-linglu-screen-recorder-basically-usable-ai-added-64-300.md) — 帖文称周末用完 3 张 Codex 重置卡、一个 Claude 账号阵亡，CapWise 达到基本可用状态，AI 新增 6.43 万行代码，产品主打开箱即用、录完一键导出。 / The post says the author used up three Codex reset cards over the weekend and lost one Claude account; CapWise (Linglu) is basically usable, AI added 64,300 lines of code, and the product emphasizes out-of-the-box use and one-click export after recording.

- [AI 走查提示词：让 Agent 主动审视产品页面与交互 / AI Walkthrough Prompt: Letting Agents Proactively Inspect Product Pages and Interactions](./sources/articles/ai-walkthrough-prompt-letting-agents-proactively-inspect-product.md) — 帖文称一段 AI 走查提示词可让 Agent 主动走查产品页面与交互细节，按六个维度判断问题严重程度并分派修复。 / The post says an AI walkthrough prompt can let agents proactively inspect product pages and interaction details, judging issue severity across six dimensions and assigning fixes.

- [Claude Code 自动记忆堆积会让智能体越用越笨，Anthropic 发布“梦境”整理帖 / Claude Code auto-memory bloat makes agents dumber over time; Anthropic publishes 'dreaming' cleanup post](./sources/articles/claude-code-auto-memory-bloat-makes-agents-dumber-over-time-anth.md) — 帖文称 Anthropic 发布工程帖，介绍 Claude 如何通过“梦境”精简和纠正自身记忆，并提供可发给 Claude Code 的提示词来清理项目自动内存。 / The post says Anthropic published an engineering post on how Claude slims and corrects its own memory via 'dreaming,' and provides a prompt to have Claude Code clean up a project's auto memory.

- [Vox 发文推荐 AGENTS.md 加入六条省 Token 准则 / Vox recommends adding six token-saving lines to AGENTS.md](./sources/articles/vox-recommends-adding-six-token-saving-lines-to-agents-md.md) — 帖文称在 AGENTS.md 中加入六条准则，可避免 Claude Code 浪费 Token。 / The post recommends adding six rules to AGENTS.md to prevent Claude Code from burning through tokens.

- [Vox 呼吁 vibe 编码者让 AI 挖掘隐式状态机并统筹 UX、DX、AX / Vox urges vibe coders to have AI uncover implicit state machines and balance UX, DX, and AX](./sources/articles/vox-urges-vibe-coders-to-have-ai-uncover-implicit-state-machines.md) — 帖文称，应在最混乱的项目中让 Claude Code 或 Codex 寻找隐式状态机，并始终兼顾用户、开发者与智能体体验且不破坏现有功能。 / The post urges users to ask Claude Code or Codex to find implicit state machines in their messiest project while always elevating UX, DX, and AX without breaking anything.

- [Logan Kilpatrick：丰富智能渗透物理世界，通过代码加速工程 / Logan Kilpatrick: Abundant Intelligence Permeates Physical World, Accelerating Engineering via Code](./sources/articles/logan-kilpatrick-abundant-intelligence-permeates-physical-world.md) — 帖文称，丰富智能渗透物理世界并通过代码加速工程，将带来更安全汽车、更便宜住房、更耐用产品等变化。 / The post claims that abundant intelligence permeating the physical world and accelerating engineering via code will bring safer cars, less expensive homes, and more durable products.

- [卡帕蒂用 Claude Code 三分钟做出 TristanTube，两小时过滤出 HackerTube / Karpathy Builds TristanTube in Three Minutes with Claude Code and Filters HackerTube in Two Hours](./sources/articles/karpathy-builds-tristantube-in-three-minutes-with-claude-code-an.md) — 帖文称卡帕蒂用 Claude Code 一键生成收录约 4.7 万条视频的 TristanTube，并用 Claude Opus 5.5 把 20 年约 4300 万条 Hacker News 评论筛选为 6454 个视频的 HackerTube。 / The post says Karpathy one-shot generated TristanTube with Claude Code, collecting about 47,000 videos, and used Claude Opus 5.5 to filter about 43 million Hacker News comments over 20 years into 6,454 videos for HackerTube.

- [数据与算力只是乘数，更聪明的 AI 方法才能更好地扩展 / Data and Compute Are Only Multipliers; Cleverer AI Methods Scale Better](./sources/articles/data-and-compute-are-only-multipliers-cleverer-ai-methods-scale.md) — 帖文称，数据规模和算力规模只是泛化能力的乘数，更聪明的方法才能更好地扩展。 / The post says data and compute scaling are only multipliers of generalization power, and cleverer approaches scale better.

- [Anthropic 数据：AI 可承担 27%–52% 科研人员时间 / Anthropic data: AI could take on 27–52% of researcher time](./sources/articles/anthropic-data-ai-could-take-on-27-52-of-researcher-time.md) — 帖文引用 Anthropic 数据称，按当前能力普及后，AI 可能承担产出一项新研究所需科研人员时间的 27%–52%，跨领域中位数为 38%。 / The post cites Anthropic data that, with current capabilities and wide adoption, AI could take on 27–52% of researcher time needed to produce a new study, with a median of 38% across fields.

- [LLM 让调查新闻进入黄金时代 / LLMs Usher in a Golden Age of Investigative Journalism](./sources/articles/llms-usher-in-a-golden-age-of-investigative-journalism.md) — 帖文称大模型可将公司、政府档案与公共数据的深度调查研究效率提升十倍。 / The post claims LLMs can 10x investigative research into company and government filings, records, and public data.

- [艾森·莫利克称近期 AI 模型事实核查能力超越人类专家 / Ethan Mollick Says Recent AI Models Surpass Human Experts in Fact-Checking](./sources/articles/ethan-mollick-says-recent-ai-models-surpass-human-experts-in-fac.md) — 帖文称近期 AI 模型在查找事实和核查声明方面已优于包括博士专家在内的人类专家，日常工作中对 AI 输出事实核验的担忧过多。 / The post argues that concerns about verifying AI output for factual errors in everyday work are excessive, citing recent models that outperform human experts, including PhD specialists, at finding facts and checking claims.

- [Aaron Levie：AI 编程杠杆正在拉大工程师产出差距 / Aaron Levie: AI coding leverage is widening engineer productivity gaps](./sources/articles/aaron-levie-ai-coding-leverage-is-widening-engineer-productivity.md) — 帖文称，在合适的智能体工作流与深度领域理解下，AI 编程可使部分场景产出提升一两个数量级，且顶尖工程师与普通工程师的差距正在持续扩大。 / The post claims that with the right agent harness and deep domain understanding, AI coding can boost output in some scenarios by one or two orders of magnitude, while the gap between top and average engineers keeps widening.

- [Anthropic 分析约 20.7 万次科研会话：数据分析与写作最重，构思与采集用得最少 / Anthropic analyzes ~207,000 research sessions: data analysis and writing dominate, ideation and collection used least](./sources/articles/anthropic-analyzes-207-000-research-sessions-data-analysis-and-w.md) — 帖文称 Anthropic 基于约 20.7 万次 Claude 科研会话分析，科学家最常用 AI 分析数据和写论文，却最少用于提出想法和收集数据。 / The post says Anthropic analyzed ~207,000 Claude research sessions; scientists use AI most for data analysis and writing papers, least for generating ideas and collecting data.

- [封号与共享账号 / Account Bans and Shared Accounts](./sources/articles/account-bans-and-shared-accounts.md) — 帖文称 Magpie 通过本机真实 Claude Code 发请求，封号与否由 Anthropic 规则决定，多人共享账号违反条款。 / The post states that Magpie sends requests via the local real Claude Code, whether accounts are banned is decided by Anthropic's rules, and sharing accounts among multiple people violates the terms.

- [magpie v0.1.3221 接入 OpenMausBot，全部模型可作为 magpie 引擎供机器人选择 / magpie v0.1.3221 integrates with OpenMausBot, all models available as magpie engine for bots](./sources/articles/magpie-v0-1-3221-integrates-with-openmausbot-all-models-availabl.md) — 帖文称 magpie v0.1.3221 已支持 OpenMausBot，在 Agents 页开启后 magpie 全部模型会作为 magpie 引擎出现在 OpenMausBot 里，供机器人选用。 / The post says magpie v0.1.3221 supports OpenMausBot; after enabling it on the Agents page, all magpie models appear as the magpie engine in OpenMausBot for bot selection.

- [Alma 远程控 Herdr / Alma Remote Controls Herdr](./sources/articles/alma-remote-controls-herdr.md) — 帖文称 Alma 0.4.486 修复了因 herdr 技能中的 HERDR_ENV=1 判断导致的操作中断，并支持打开空工作区。 / The post says Alma 0.4.486 fixes operation interruption caused by the HERDR_ENV=1 check in herdr's skill, and supports opening an empty workspace.

- [Alma 远程控 Herdr，用户称体验流畅优于传统远程桌面 / Alma remote control of Herdr reported smooth and better than traditional remote desktop](./sources/articles/alma-remote-control-of-herdr-reported-smooth-and-better-than-tra.md) — yetone 转发 Harry 关于用 Alma 远程控制 herdr 的帖文，称体验流畅且优于 uu 远程等传统方案。 / yetone reposts Harry's post about using Alma to remotely control herdr, reporting a smooth experience superior to traditional solutions like uu remote.

- [anidoodle：用纯代码绘制手绘风动画与电影 / anidoodle: Hand-drawn Animation and Film via Pure Code](./sources/articles/anidoodle-hand-drawn-animation-and-film-via-pure-code.md) — 帖文称 anidoodle 将插画、运镜与配乐全部转化为代码函数，支持 32 种手绘质感并实现确定性渲染。 / The post claims anidoodle converts illustration, camera movement, and music entirely into code functions, supporting 32 hand-drawn textures with deterministic rendering.

- [PSI 称用 AI 与 Lean 证明 Aaronson-Ambainis 猜想 / PSI Claims AI and Lean Proved the Aaronson-Ambainis Conjecture](./sources/articles/psi-claims-ai-and-lean-proved-the-aaronson-ambainis-conjecture.md) — 帖文称 PSI 通过 AI 系统和 Lean 交互式定理证明器证明了困扰量子计算近二十年的阿伦森-安拜尼斯猜想。 / The post claims PSI used an AI system and Lean interactive theorem prover to prove the Aaronson-Ambainis conjecture, which has troubled quantum computing for nearly two decades.

- [Lyra 2.0：让一张静态图片变成可自由探索的3D虚拟世界 / Lyra 2.0: Turn a Static Image into a Freely Explorable 3D Virtual World](./sources/articles/lyra-2-0-turn-a-static-image-into-a-freely-explorable-3d-virtual.md) — 帖文称英伟达开源基于 WAN-14B 的 Lyra 2.0，从单张 480×832 照片和相机轨迹生成可漫游的3D高斯点云场景。 / The post says NVIDIA's open-source WAN-14B-based Lyra 2.0 generates a roamable 3D Gaussian point-cloud scene from a single 480×832 photo and camera trajectory.

- [Anthropic 记忆博客转述：AI 智能体通过“做梦”整理经验 / Anthropic Memory Blog Retelling: AI Agents Organize Experience Through 'Dreaming'](./sources/articles/anthropic-memory-blog-retelling-ai-agents-organize-experience-th.md) — 帖文称 Anthropic 提出让 AI 智能体在空闲时离线回看记录、纠错并生成 wiki，以提升多任务记忆准确率并节省 Token。 / The post says Anthropic proposes letting AI agents offline-review records, correct errors, and generate wikis during idle time to improve multi-task memory accuracy and save tokens.

- [深圳 GEO 嘉宾谭少卿：Floatboat 创始人分享如何打造超级 Agent / Shenzhen GEO Guest Tan Shaoqing: Floatboat Founder on Building a Super Agent](./sources/articles/shenzhen-geo-guest-tan-shaoqing-floatboat-founder-on-building-a.md) — 谭少卿介绍 Floatboat 面向独立创作者的 all-in-one AI 工作空间，并将在 GEO 大会现场教手搓工作台打通 GEO 内容发布。 / Tan Shaoqing introduces Floatboat, an all-in-one AI workspace for independent creators, and will teach attendees to hand-build a workbench connecting GEO content publishing at the Shenzhen GEO conference.

- [Capwise 第一版基本完成 / Capwise first version basically complete](./sources/articles/capwise-first-version-basically-complete.md) — 开源录屏工具 Capwise 首个版本基本完成，计划下周末开源。 / The first version of open-source screen recording tool Capwise is basically complete, with open-source planned for next weekend.

- [蓝哥AI分享万字出海方法论：普通人如何用AI构建海外美金收入 / Lange AI Shares 10K-Word Overseas Methodology: How Ordinary People Use AI to Build USD Income Abroad](./sources/articles/lange-ai-shares-10k-word-overseas-methodology-how-ordinary-peopl.md) — 蓝哥AI在X上推荐一篇万字出海指南，拆解三大套利逻辑与五大赛道，给出启动成本与见效周期对照表。 / Lange AI recommends a 10K-word overseas guide on X, breaking down three arbitrage logics and five tracks with a startup cost and feedback cycle comparison table.

- [Clothesline-ui：Codex 技能把待办与稍后阅读挂在 Mac 屏幕边缘 / Clothesline-ui: Codex skill hangs to-dos and read-later items on Mac screen edge](./sources/articles/clothesline-ui-codex-skill-hangs-to-dos-and-read-later-items-on.md) — 龙海开源的 Codex Skill clothesline-ui 让待办、灵感和稍后阅读以 Mac 屏幕边缘“晾衣杆”形式展开，不用时完全隐藏。 / Longhai's Codex Skill clothesline-ui presents to-dos, inspiration, and read-later items as a Mac screen-edge 'clothesline' that stays hidden until the mouse hovers.

- [X 原创收益已双倍回本 Grok Ultra 年费 / X Original Earnings Already Double Paid Back Grok Ultra Annual Fee](./sources/articles/x-original-earnings-already-double-paid-back-grok-ultra-annual-f.md) — 帖文称 X 原创收益超 1000 美元，已覆盖约 1000 美元的 Grok Ultra 年费并实现两倍回本。 / The post claims X creator earnings exceeded $1,000, covering the roughly $1,000 Grok Ultra annual fee and achieving double payback.

- [世界一下就切换到了禁止 AI 的时间线 / The World Suddenly Switched to a Timeline Banning AI](./sources/articles/the-world-suddenly-switched-to-a-timeline-banning-ai.md) — 帖文称美国民主党议员提议暂停人工超级智能研发，引发市场恐慌，并讨论 AI 监管、算力泡沫与普通人未获技术红利等问题。 / The post claims a US Democratic lawmaker proposed suspending artificial superintelligence development, triggering market panic and discussion on AI regulation, compute bubbles, and ordinary people not receiving AI dividends.

- [《世界一下就切换到了禁止 AI 的时间线》—— 橘 AI 对超级 AI 禁令的理性复盘 / "World Switched to AI-Ban Timeline Overnight" — Orange AI's Rational Take on Super-AI Prohibition](./sources/articles/world-switched-to-ai-ban-timeline-overnight-orange-ai-s-rational.md) — 橘 AI 作者针对近期从 AI 基建狂热反转为超级 AI 禁令的现象，提出三点核心观点：大力发展 AI 但不制造泡沫、重视超级 AI 安全、区分当前替代价值与未来增量价值。 / Orange AI author responds to the sudden shift from AI infrastructure mania to super-AI bans with three tenets: develop AI vigorously without blowing bubbles, prioritize super-AI safety, and distinguish today's replacement value from tomorrow's incremental value.

- [Claude 会做动画，不代表每个 UI 元素都要加动画 / Claude Can Animate, But Not Every UI Element Needs Animation](./sources/articles/claude-can-animate-but-not-every-ui-element-needs-animation.md) — 小邓同学在 X 上吐槽：Claude 会做动画，不代表每个按钮、卡片和页面切换都要加花哨动画。 / Xiaodong Xue complains on X that just because Claude can create animations does not mean every button, card, and page transition needs flashy animation.

- [Claude 工期与动画梗 / Claude Work Estimates and Animation Meme](./sources/articles/claude-work-estimates-and-animation-meme.md) — 帖文称 Claude 认真估算需 2～3 天，实际 2 分钟就完成。 / The post claims Claude seriously estimated 2–3 days but actually finished in 2 minutes.

- [旗舰晚发或许在抢规则权 / Delayed Flagship Releases May Be a Bid for Rule-Making Power](./sources/articles/delayed-flagship-releases-may-be-a-bid-for-rule-making-power.md) — 帖文称 OpenAI 与 Anthropic 推迟通用旗舰模型，或在借安全议题与受控发布争夺 AI 行业规则制定权。 / The post claims OpenAI and Anthropic are delaying general flagship models, possibly competing for AI industry rule-making power through safety issues and controlled releases.

- [很多 AI Agent 本质是绑信用卡的 cron job / Many AI Agents Are Essentially Cron Jobs with Credit Cards](./sources/articles/many-ai-agents-are-essentially-cron-jobs-with-credit-cards.md) — 研究者调查 3.6 万 GitHub 仓库发现：Agent 触发器众多却缺检查与退出机制，不知任务对错却懂持续扣费。 / Study of 36k GitHub repos finds agents have many triggers but few checks/exits — they don't know if tasks succeed but know how to keep charging.

- [对 AI 内容疲劳，想转当吃喝视频博主 / Tired of AI content, wants to become a food and drink vlogger](./sources/articles/tired-of-ai-content-wants-to-become-a-food-and-drink-vlogger.md) — 用户在 X 发帖称厌倦满屏 AI，想像隋坡一样做全国吃喝视频博主，并好奇小时候学相声或当厨子的人生会如何。 / The user posts on X saying they are tired of AI-filled screens and want to become a food vlogger like Suibo, wondering what life would be like if they had learned crosstalk or become a chef.

- [推荐 Lattice：本地 LaTeX 科研工作区 / Recommending Lattice: A Local LaTeX Research Workspace](./sources/articles/recommending-lattice-a-local-latex-research-workspace.md) — 帖文称 Lattice 是面向科研的本地 LaTeX 工作区，整合论文、笔记、幻灯片、Overleaf 协作者与 AI 代理，免费开源下载。 / The post recommends Lattice, a free open-source local LaTeX workspace for research that integrates papers, notes, slides, Overleaf collaborators, and an AI agent.

- [今后 A 社可能不会封号了 / Post claims company A may stop banning accounts](./sources/articles/post-claims-company-a-may-stop-banning-accounts.md) — 帖文称 OpenAI 近期出现大规模封号，并将此与某员工此前跳槽 Cursor 后限制中国用户使用 Claude 联系起来。 / The post claims OpenAI recently saw mass account bans and links them to an employee who previously joined Cursor, after which China users were restricted from using Claude.

- [Grok Bot 浏览器插件通过 Chrome 应用商店审核 / Grok Bot browser extension passes Chrome Web Store review](./sources/articles/grok-bot-browser-extension-passes-chrome-web-store-review.md) — 铁柱AGI 的 Grok Bot 浏览器插件已通过 Chrome 审核，支持将网页截图、元素、整页及 X、小红书、抖音、公众号内容发送至指定 Grok Bot。 / Tiezhu AGI's Grok Bot browser extension has passed Chrome review, supporting sending webpage screenshots, elements, full pages, and X, Xiaohongshu, Douyin, and Official Account content to specified Grok Bots.

- [Uber Agent 安全实践：有权限执行，不代表符合用户本意 / Uber Agent Security Practice: Permission to Execute Does Not Mean It Matches User Intent](./sources/articles/uber-agent-security-practice-permission-to-execute-does-not-mean.md) — Uber Secure AI 通过上线前审查、运行时约束和持续测试，核对智能体动作是否真正源自用户请求，而非仅依赖传统权限，但未公布量化防护数据。 / Uber Secure AI verifies whether agent actions truly originate from user requests through pre-deployment review, runtime constraints, and continuous testing, rather than relying solely on traditional permissions, without publishing quantitative protection data.

- [Airbnb 小模型以约 1% 推理成本匹敌前沿模型分类水平 / Airbnb Small Model Matches Frontier Model Classification at ~1% Inference Cost](./sources/articles/airbnb-small-model-matches-frontier-model-classification-at-1-in.md) — Airbnb 实测显示，针对高频内容审核任务微调的约 20 亿参数小模型，分类效果接近前沿模型，延迟低于 100 毫秒，高流量下推理成本约为前沿 API 的 1%。 / Airbnb's real-world test shows a fine-tuned ~2B-parameter small model matches frontier model classification for high-frequency content moderation, with sub-100ms latency and about 1% of frontier API inference cost at high traffic.

- [Dreaming：记忆要能纠错 / Dreaming: Memory Must Be Correctable](./sources/articles/dreaming-memory-must-be-correctable.md) — Anthropic 的“dreaming”机制让 Claude 在任务之外通过离线计算回顾经历、纠正错误并重组外部记忆，而非更新模型权重。 / Anthropic's 'dreaming' mechanism lets Claude review experiences, correct errors, and reorganize external memory outside tasks via offline computation, without updating model weights.

- [Claude Duet 接上 Claude Code，手机可审批与调度编程任务 / Claude Duet Connects to Claude Code; Phone Can Approve and Schedule Coding Tasks](./sources/articles/claude-duet-connects-to-claude-code-phone-can-approve-and-schedu.md) — 帖文称，Anthropic 为个人 AI Agent Claude Duet 接入 Claude Code，支持从手机查看、审批并协调电脑或云端的编程任务。 / The post says Anthropic has connected Claude Code to its personal AI agent Claude Duet, supporting viewing, approving, and coordinating computer or cloud coding tasks from a phone.

- [Claude Code 将登录邮箱写入 User-Agent 并外泄 / Claude Code leaks login email via User-Agent](./sources/articles/claude-code-leaks-login-email-via-user-agent.md) — Claude Code 把用户登录邮箱放入模型上下文，导致模型在联网请求中自行填入真实邮箱并发送给 Wikipedia、SEC 等网站；Anthropic 称正在修复但方案尚不彻底。 / Claude Code put users' login emails into the model context, causing the model to fill in real emails in web requests and send them to sites such as Wikipedia and the SEC; Anthropic says it is fixing the issue but the solution is not yet thorough.

- [Claude 的 Agent 长期记忆与「做梦」机制 / Claude's Agent Long-Term Memory and "Dreaming" Mechanism](./sources/articles/claude-s-agent-long-term-memory-and-dreaming-mechanism.md) — Anthropic 研究揭示 Claude 如何通过四层记忆和任务间隙的「做梦」整理经验、纠错并建立 Wiki。 / Anthropic research reveals how Claude organizes experience, corrects errors, and builds a Wiki through four-layer memory and "dreaming" between tasks.

- [GPT 封号潮预案：作者称 DeepSeek Harness 支持多模型切换与迁移 / Contingency plan for GPT ban wave: author says DeepSeek Harness supports multi-model switching and migration](./sources/articles/contingency-plan-for-gpt-ban-wave-author-says-deepseek-harness-s.md) — 帖文称 GPT、Claude 接连出现封号风波，建议提前导出数据并考虑 DeepSeek Harness 等国产模型框架做迁移准备。 / The post says GPT and Claude have faced consecutive account ban waves, advising users to export data early and consider DeepSeek Harness and other domestic model frameworks for migration preparation.

- [GPT 封号潮预案：建议先导出 ChatGPT 数据备份 / GPT Ban Wave Contingency: Export ChatGPT Data First](./sources/articles/gpt-ban-wave-contingency-export-chatgpt-data-first.md) — 帖文称 GPT 可能迎来类似 Claude 的封号潮，建议用户通过网页端设置导出数据并备份重要对话。 / The post claims GPT may face a ban wave similar to Claude and advises users to export data via web settings and back up important conversations.

- [DeepSeek Harness 插件需自行实现自动更新机制 / DeepSeek Harness Plugin Requires Self-Implemented Auto-Update Mechanism](./sources/articles/deepseek-harness-plugin-requires-self-implemented-auto-update-me.md) — 宝玉在 X 上表示，开发 DeepSeek Harness 插件时不得不自己实现插件自动更新机制。 / Bao Yu said on X that developing a DeepSeek Harness plugin required implementing the plugin auto-update mechanism himself.

- [一句话建提示词库 / One-sentence prompt library](./sources/articles/one-sentence-prompt-library.md) — 用户通过 Grok Bot 一句话建立了个人图片和视频模型提示词库。 / The user built a personal image and video model prompt library with one sentence via Grok Bot.

- [Muse 视频模型免费试用引热议 / Muse Video Model Free Trial Draws Attention](./sources/articles/muse-video-model-free-trial-draws-attention.md) — 帖文称 Muse 视频模型近期走红，因首次免费试用此类高端模型，在真实场景中表现离谱，而同等级模型仍昂贵。 / The post says the Muse video model has recently become popular, performing outstandingly in real scenarios because users can try this high-end model for free for the first time, while similar models remain expensive.

- [baoyu-design：将 Claude Artifact 类型带到本地智能体的技能集合 / baoyu-design: Skills Bringing Claude Artifact Types to Local Agents](./sources/articles/baoyu-design-skills-bringing-claude-artifact-types-to-local-agen.md) — 一个 GitHub 仓库提供 7 个技能，让本地编程智能体能创建可编辑的幻灯片、设计画布、设计系统、数据仪表板和动态影片，并在本地编辑器中协作。 / A GitHub repo providing 7 skills that let local coding agents create editable slide decks, design canvases, design systems, data dashboards, and motion films, with local editors for collaboration.

- [宝玉：Baoyu Design Skill DeepSeek Harness 插件新增统一入口 / Bao Yu: Baoyu Design Skill DeepSeek Harness Plugin Adds Unified Entry](./sources/articles/bao-yu-baoyu-design-skill-deepseek-harness-plugin-adds-unified-e.md) — 宝玉更新了 Baoyu Design Skill 的 DeepSeek Harness 插件，提供 Slides、Design、Dashboard、Motion 结果的统一入口及会话发起功能。 / Bao Yu updated the Baoyu Design Skill DeepSeek Harness plugin to provide a unified entry for Slides, Design, Dashboard, and Motion results and session initiation.

- [Cat Wu 离开 Anthropic / Cat Wu Leaves Anthropic](./sources/articles/cat-wu-leaves-anthropic.md) — Claude Code 产品负责人 Cat Wu 宣布离开 Anthropic，未透露离职原因及下一步去向。 / Claude Code product lead Cat Wu announces her departure from Anthropic without disclosing the reason or her next destination.

- [MCP 按 Agent 勾选省上下文 / MCP Per-Agent Checkbox Saves Context](./sources/articles/mcp-per-agent-checkbox-saves-context.md) — Magpie 仅将勾选的 MCP 服务器下发给对应 Agent，避免每次请求携带全部工具定义，从而节省上下文。 / Magpie sends only the MCP servers checked for each Agent, avoiding full tool definitions on every request and saving context.

- [Magpie 靠 Alma 开发 / Magpie Developed via Alma](./sources/articles/magpie-developed-via-alma.md) — yetone 称最近 Magpie 都是通过 Alma 开发，需先把 Alma 打磨好才能快速迭代 / yetone says Magpie is recently developed via Alma, and Alma must be polished first for Magpie to iterate quickly

- [Alma Personal Agent 上线 / Alma Personal Agent Launched](./sources/articles/alma-personal-agent-launched.md) — yetone 在 X 上发布帖文称 Alma Personal Agent 已上线。 / yetone posts on X that Alma Personal Agent has launched.

- [Rauchg 转发 AI CLI：Agent 可统一调用图音视频生成 / Rauchg boosts AI CLI: agents unified access to image, audio, and video generation](./sources/articles/rauchg-boosts-ai-cli-agents-unified-access-to-image-audio-and-vi.md) — Guillermo Rauch 转发 Chris Tate 的 AI CLI，称其让每个 Agent 都能使用视频、语音、图像与文本生成，并通过 AI Gateway 实现成本与零数据留存控制。 / Guillermo Rauch boosted Chris Tate's AI CLI, saying it lets every agent use video, voice, image, and text generation, with cost and zero-data-retention controls via AI Gateway.

- [宝玉：AI 时代应更关注产品细节而非代码整洁 / Dotey: In the AI era, focus more on product details than code cleanliness](./sources/articles/dotey-in-the-ai-era-focus-more-on-product-details-than-code-clea.md) — 宝玉认为 AI 时代应将更多精力放在用户体验、性能和安全等产品细节上，代码细节因 Agent 编码而变得不那么重要。 / Dotey argues that in the AI era, more effort should go into product details like UX, performance, and security, while code details matter less when Agents write code.

- [Matt Pocock 称有人为其 /retro 技能撰写效果评估论文 / Matt Pocock says someone wrote a paper assessing his /retro skill's effectiveness](./sources/articles/matt-pocock-says-someone-wrote-a-paper-assessing-his-retro-skill.md) — Matt Pocock 在 X 上发文称有人为其 /retro 技能撰写效果评估论文，并分享 arXiv 链接。 / Matt Pocock posted on X that someone wrote a paper assessing his /retro skill's effectiveness and shared an arXiv link.

- [Hamel Husain 指出公开基准存在评测缺陷，主张私有评测 / Hamel Husain highlights public benchmark flaws, advocates private evals](./sources/articles/hamel-husain-highlights-public-benchmark-flaws-advocates-private.md) — Hamel Husain 转发 BenchmarkGate 揭露，指出 AutomationBench 与 OfficeQA Pro v2 存在评分器缺陷与答案泄露问题，建议勿轻信第三方指标而应自建私有评测。 / Hamel Husain shares a BenchmarkGate reveal showing AutomationBench and OfficeQA Pro v2 have grader flaws and answer-key leakage, urging reliance on private evals over third-party metrics.

- [Claude Code 子代理默认跑主模型，建议强制切 Haiku 省 Opus 额度 / Claude Code subagents default to main model; force Haiku to save Opus quota](./sources/articles/claude-code-subagents-default-to-main-model-force-haiku-to-save.md) — 帖文称 Claude Code 的 Explore 与 Plan 子代理默认使用主模型，需设置 FORCE=1 才能全部切换到 Haiku，避免烧掉 Opus 额度。 / The post says Claude Code's Explore and Plan subagents run on the main model by default, and FORCE=1 is needed to switch all subagents to Haiku to avoid burning Opus quota.

- [AI 编程 Agent 的 /retro 复盘与技能自我进化实证讨论 / Empirical Discussion on /retro Retrospection and AI Coding Agent Skill Self-Evolution](./sources/articles/empirical-discussion-on-retro-retrospection-and-ai-coding-agent.md) — 实证研究发现，AI 编程 Agent 任务后复盘并修改技能文件，仅少数改动能彻底消除问题，部分改动还会增加工作量。 / Empirical study finds that post-task retrospection and skill-file edits by AI coding agents eliminate the target problem in only a few cases, and some changes increase workload.

- [Fireworks 每日服务超 40T 代币的“专用模型” / Fireworks Serves Over 40 Trillion Tokens Daily of "Specialized Models"](./sources/articles/fireworks-serves-over-40-trillion-tokens-daily-of-specialized-mo.md) — Nathan Lambert 在 X 上发帖称 Fireworks 每日服务超过 40 万亿代币的“专用模型”，并将其定义为面向客户的后训练模型。 / Nathan Lambert posted on X that Fireworks serves more than 40 trillion tokens per day of "specialized models," defining them as any post-trained model for a customer.

- [西蒙·威利森：Qwen 3.8 27B 在笔记本上也能解读模型权重 / Simon Willison: Qwen 3.8 27B Can Interpret Model Weights on a Laptop Too](./sources/articles/simon-willison-qwen-3-8-27b-can-interpret-model-weights-on-a-lap.md) — 帖文称 Qwen 3.8 27B 在本地运行 45 分钟后，可实现与新论文所述 GPT-6 Astra 类似的模型权重直接解读能力。 / The post claims Qwen 3.8 27B, running locally for over 45 minutes, can directly interpret model weights similar to what a new paper describes for GPT-6 Astra.

- [Ethan Mollick：新模型复刻旧发现，常规认知已过时 / Ethan Mollick: New Models Make Old LLM Findings Outdated](./sources/articles/ethan-mollick-new-models-make-old-llm-findings-outdated.md) — Ethan Mollick 指出，新模型不再编造引用，幻觉与刻板印象下降，但仍存在奉承与左倾倾向。 / Ethan Mollick says newer models no longer invent citations, with lower hallucination and stereotyping, but remain sycophantic and left-leaning.

- [当智能体执行工作时保持批判性思维 / Critical thinking when agents do the work](./sources/articles/critical-thinking-when-agents-do-the-work.md) — 保持批判性思维，避免在代理工作流中陷入认知投降 / Maintain critical thinking and avoid cognitive surrender in agentic workflows

- [Matt Pocock 提出本地编排器与软件工厂组合构思 / Matt Pocock proposes combining a local orchestrator with a software factory](./sources/articles/matt-pocock-proposes-combining-a-local-orchestrator-with-a-softw.md) — Matt Pocock 设想以本地长期运行编排器对接软件工厂，通过 CLI 与自然语言驱动 Agent 线程、复盘与定时任务。 / Matt Pocock envisions connecting a local, long-running orchestrator to a software factory, using CLI and plain language to drive agent threads, retrospectives, and cron jobs.

- [Lenny Rachitsky：AI 将建筑施工图绘制从 1.5 年缩短至 8 分钟 / Lenny Rachitsky: AI cuts architectural construction drawing work from 1.5 years to 8 minutes](./sources/articles/lenny-rachitsky-ai-cuts-architectural-construction-drawing-work.md) — 帖文称，一位经验丰富的建筑师表示，借助 AI，将建筑设计转为施工图的工作从 1.5 年缩短至 8 分钟。 / The post says an experienced architect reported that AI reduced turning architectural designs into construction-ready drawings from 1.5 years to 8 minutes.

- [Vercel 发布 Security Review 公测，安全 Agent 自动审计每一行代码 / Vercel Releases Security Review Public Beta, Security Agent Automatically Audits Every Line of Code](./sources/articles/vercel-releases-security-review-public-beta-security-agent-autom.md) — Vercel 面向所有代码仓库推出公测版 Security Review，由安全团队开发的 Agent 自动审计并一键修复漏洞。 / Vercel releases public beta Security Review for all code repositories, with a security-team-built Agent that automatically audits and fixes vulnerabilities in one click.

- [Amjad Masad：员工终端上的 AI 智能体成为系统性安全风险 / Amjad Masad: AI agents on employee endpoints become systemic security risk](./sources/articles/amjad-masad-ai-agents-on-employee-endpoints-become-systemic-secu.md) — Amjad Masad 发布安全通告，指出在员工终端运行的 AI 智能体对企业和政府构成系统性安全风险，建议将托管云环境作为 AI 智能体的默认部署方式。 / Amjad Masad issues a security advisory warning that AI agents running on employee endpoints pose systemic security risks to enterprises and governments, recommending managed cloud environments as the default for AI agents.

- [Aaron Levie：AI 产出分化达历史最高，生产力上限跃升至 5-10 倍 / Aaron Levie: AI output divergence at historic high, productivity ceiling jumps to 5-10x](./sources/articles/aaron-levie-ai-output-divergence-at-historic-high-productivity-c.md) — Aaron Levie 指出 AI 采用最广但产出分化最大，前沿模型与智能体编排使部分领域生产力上限从 30-50% 提升至 5-10 倍。 / Aaron Levie notes AI adoption is broadest ever while output divergence is highest, with frontier models and agent orchestration raising productivity ceilings from 30-50% to 5-10x in some fields.

- [Claude 企业插件自动连接 / Claude Auto-connects Enterprise Plugins via MCP Trusted Issuer](./sources/articles/claude-auto-connects-enterprise-plugins-via-mcp-trusted-issuer.md) — Claude 团队与企业管理员可一次性配置公司插件，队友首次使用时自动连接，无需逐一重新授权。 / Claude Team and Enterprise admins can configure company plugins once, and teammates are auto-connected on first use without re-authorizing each time.

- [cat（@_catwu）离开 Anthropic，回顾 Claude Code 从内部工具到行业定义产品的两年 / cat (@_catwu) leaves Anthropic, reflecting on Claude Code's two-year journey from internal tool to industry-defining product](./sources/articles/cat-catwu-leaves-anthropic-reflecting-on-claude-code-s-two-year.md) — 一名前 Anthropic 员工宣布离职，并回顾 Claude Code 如何从小型内部工具发展为用户信任其自动审批并执行大规模迁移与新产品开发的行业定义产品。 / A former Anthropic employee announces departure and reflects on how Claude Code evolved from a small internal tool into an industry-defining product that users trust to auto-approve and execute large migrations and new product builds.

- [OpenAI 宣布下一轮内部安全冲刺，提升漏洞赏金并与 Cathedral Systems 合作 / OpenAI Announces Next Security Sprint, Raises Bug Bounties, Partners with Cathedral Systems](./sources/articles/openai-announces-next-security-sprint-raises-bug-bounties-partne.md) — OpenAI 计划与 Cathedral Systems 合作使用 AI 模型挑战自身防御，并大幅提高漏洞赏金奖励。 / OpenAI plans to partner with Cathedral Systems to use AI models to challenge its defenses and significantly increase bug bounty rewards.

- [OpenAI 称 ChatGPT 与 Codex 助力工程师构建物理世界 / OpenAI Says ChatGPT and Codex Help Engineers Build for the Physical World](./sources/articles/openai-says-chatgpt-and-codex-help-engineers-build-for-the-physi.md) — OpenAI 发帖称 ChatGPT 和 Codex 正在帮助工程师在机械设计、电气工程和仿真等领域构建物理世界。 / OpenAI posted that ChatGPT and Codex are helping engineers build for the physical world across mechanical design, electrical engineering, and simulation.

- [FlashProve：无需泄露权重即证明模型输出 / FlashProve: Prove model output without revealing weights](./sources/articles/flashprove-prove-model-output-without-revealing-weights.md) — Anthropics 的 FlashProve 让 AI 提供商能在不泄露模型权重的情况下，证明回复来自承诺的模型。 / Anthropics' FlashProve lets AI providers prove a reply came from the committed model without revealing model weights.

- [大语言模型可证明推理的进展 / Progress on provable inference for large language models](./sources/articles/progress-on-provable-inference-for-large-language-models.md) — Anthropic 发布 FlashProve，利用 zkSNARK 实现大语言模型推理的零知识简洁论证，在不泄露模型权重的前提下验证输出是否由指定模型生成。 / Anthropic releases FlashProve, a zkSNARK-based system for provable inference of large language models, enabling verification that outputs were generated by a specific model without revealing model weights.

- [Anthropic 发布 FlashProve：面向大语言模型的可证明推理研究 / Anthropic releases FlashProve: provable inference research for large language models](./sources/articles/anthropic-releases-flashprove-provable-inference-research-for-la.md) — Anthropic 介绍 FlashProve，利用密码学技术验证 AI 回复是否来自指定模型，并开源相关代码。 / Anthropic introduces FlashProve, using cryptographic techniques to verify whether an AI response came from a given model, and open-sources the related code.

- [FlashProve：零知识证明验证 AI 回答来源模型 / FlashProve: Zero-knowledge proof verifies which AI model generated an answer](./sources/articles/flashprove-zero-knowledge-proof-verifies-which-ai-model-generate.md) — Anthropic 开源 FlashProve，利用零知识证明与 Lean 4 形式化验证，让用户在不获取模型权重的情况下确认 AI 回答来自指定模型。 / Anthropic open-sources FlashProve, using zero-knowledge proofs and Lean 4 formal verification to let users confirm AI answers come from a specified model without accessing model weights.

- [知了 · Cicada：Mac 实时双语会议纪要与同声传译工具 / Cicada (知了): Mac Real-time Bilingual Meeting Minutes and Simultaneous Interpretation Tool](./sources/articles/cicada-mac-real-time-bilingual-meeting-minutes-and-simultaneous.md) — 一款仅在 Mac 上运行的开源实时会议工具，提供双语字幕、结构化纪要与同声传译，声音处理主要在本机完成。 / An open-source Mac-only real-time meeting tool offering bilingual subtitles, structured minutes, and simultaneous interpretation, with audio processing done mostly on-device.

- [Anthropic S-1 算力账 / Anthropic S-1: The $895 Billion Compute Bet](./sources/articles/anthropic-s-1-the-895-billion-compute-bet.md) — 帖文称 Anthropic 营收将从 2025 年 Q1 的 3.76 亿美元激增至 2026 年 Q3 的 170 亿美元，并已签署高达 8950 亿美元的算力义务。 / The post claims Anthropic's revenue will surge from $376 million in 2025 Q1 to $17 billion in 2026 Q3, with signed compute obligations reaching $895 billion.

- [LLM「常识」15 个月保质期 / LLM "Common Sense" 15-Month Shelf Life](./sources/articles/llm-common-sense-15-month-shelf-life.md) — 斯坦福研究重测 50 条广泛引用的 LLM 结论，44% 已失效、34% 减弱，仅 16% 仍成立。 / Stanford re-test of 50 widely cited LLM conclusions: 44% invalidated, 34% weakened, only 16% still valid.

- [超喜欢 Amp 的设计与大部分使用体验 / Really like Amp's design and most of the usage experience](./sources/articles/really-like-amp-s-design-and-most-of-the-usage-experience.md) — 用户在 X 上发帖称非常喜欢 Amp 的设计和大部分使用体验，但以“除了……”收尾，未展开具体不满。 / User posts on X saying they really like Amp's design and most of the usage experience, ending with “except…” without elaborating on specific complaints.

- [大佬切 T3 / Big shots switch to T3](./sources/articles/big-shots-switch-to-t3.md) — 用户 @dingyi 观察到多位大佬切换到 T3 并盛赞，认为此前评测属实，自身尚未体验。 / User @dingyi observes multiple experts switching to T3 and praising it, finds earlier reviews credible, and says they have not tried it.

- [知了 / Cicada：Mac 开源会议纪要与同声传译软件 / ZhiLe / Cicada: Open-source Mac meeting minutes and simultaneous interpretation software](./sources/articles/zhile-cicada-open-source-mac-meeting-minutes-and-simultaneous-in.md) — 知了 / Cicada 是一款基于 Apache-2.0 协议开源的 Mac 会议纪要与同声传译软件，支持实时字幕翻译、会议摘要与声纹克隆传译。 / ZhiLe / Cicada is an Apache-2.0 licensed open-source Mac app for meeting minutes and simultaneous interpretation, supporting real-time subtitle translation, meeting summaries, and voice-cloned interpretation.

- [Revolut 默认智谱 GLM 作为 AI 编程模型 / Revolut adopts Zhipu GLM as default AI coding model](./sources/articles/revolut-adopts-zhipu-glm-as-default-ai-coding-model.md) — Revolut 将智谱 GLM 设为约 2000 名工程师的默认 AI 编程模型，Claude Code 与 Codex 退居备选，自建推理成本降至此前约十分之一。 / Revolut sets Zhipu GLM as the default AI coding model for about 2,000 engineers, demoting Claude Code and Codex to exception-only use while cutting inference cost to about one-tenth of prior Anthropic/OpenAI spend.

- [iPhone-use：免安装、零注入的 iPhone 屏幕投射与蓝牙输入操控 / iPhone-use: Install-free, injection-free iPhone screen mirroring and Bluetooth input control](./sources/articles/iphone-use-install-free-injection-free-iphone-screen-mirroring-a.md) — 该项目通过 Mac USB 投屏和蓝牙模拟输入，在手机端零安装的情况下实现对 iPhone 的操控，并声称是目前最好用的 iPhone Use 方案。 / The project mirrors iPhone screen via Mac USB and simulates Bluetooth input to control iPhone with zero installation on the phone, and is claimed to be the currently best iPhone Use solution.

- [iPhone-use 开源：AI 经 Mac USB 投屏与蓝牙输入操作 iPhone / iPhone-use Open-Source: AI Operates iPhone via Mac USB Mirroring and Bluetooth Input](./sources/articles/iphone-use-open-source-ai-operates-iphone-via-mac-usb-mirroring.md) — 开发者 YueChen 开源 iPhone-use，通过 USB 画面传输与 Mac 蓝牙模拟输入，接入 MCP 后 AI 可查看屏幕、点击与输入，并经改造支持本地 OCR、增量差异与中文输入。 / Developer YueChen open-sourced iPhone-use, mirroring iPhone screen to Mac over USB and simulating input via Bluetooth; after MCP integration, AI can view, click, and input, with modifications adding local OCR, incremental diffs, and Chinese input.

- [字节限制外部 AI 工具访问内部数据 / ByteDance Restricts External AI Tools from Accessing Internal Data](./sources/articles/bytedance-restricts-external-ai-tools-from-accessing-internal-da.md) — 字节跳动计划从 11 月起限制 Claude、Codex 等外部 AI 工具访问 L4 代码仓库和飞书文档，个人购买的 AI 账号也将失效。 / ByteDance plans to restrict external AI tools such as Claude and Codex from accessing L4 code repositories and Feishu documents starting November, and personally purchased AI accounts will also lose access.

- [扣子企业版下线时间表 / Coze Enterprise Edition Phase-Out Timeline](./sources/articles/coze-enterprise-edition-phase-out-timeline.md) — 字节跳动旗下 AI Agent 平台扣子（Coze）宣布分阶段下线企业版，新版企业套餐最晚于 2027 年 12 月 31 日全面下线。 / ByteDance's AI Agent platform Coze announces a phased phase-out of its enterprise edition, with new enterprise packages fully discontinued by December 31, 2027.

- [AI 进交易决策链 / AI Enters the Trading Decision Chain](./sources/articles/ai-enters-the-trading-decision-chain.md) — 帖文称 AI 正悄然渗透专家与投资者的交易决策，引发对人类判断力是否可保留的担忧。 / The post claims AI is quietly infiltrating expert and investor trading decisions, raising concerns over whether human judgment can be retained.

- [RSI 初现：无需训练直接修改模型权重 / RSI Emerges: Directly Editing Model Weights Without Training](./sources/articles/rsi-emerges-directly-editing-model-weights-without-training.md) — 帖文称通过“权重空间编程”仅修改 Qwen3.5-0.8B 0.016% 的权重，即可使数学能力提升六倍且无需梯度或训练。 / The post claims that “Weight-Space Programming” modified only 0.016% of Qwen3.5-0.8B’s weights to improve math up to 6x without gradients or training.

- [AI 短剧边看评论边改剧本，帖文称可挑战奈飞 / AI short dramas adjust scripts based on comments, post claims they can challenge Netflix](./sources/articles/ai-short-dramas-adjust-scripts-based-on-comments-post-claims-the.md) — 用户@vista8 发帖称 AI 短剧可边看评论反馈边改剧本，低成本高产出，传统影视团队难以抗衡 / User @vista8 posts that AI short dramas can adjust scripts based on comment feedback, with low cost and high output, making it hard for traditional film and TV teams to compete

- [少说废话省 Token：DeepSeek Harness 优化 Agent 流程 / Cut the Chatter to Save Tokens: DeepSeek Harness Optimizes Agent Workflow](./sources/articles/cut-the-chatter-to-save-tokens-deepseek-harness-optimizes-agent.md) — 主张让 AI Agent 少说废话，通过编译/测试/下一步的自动询问流程，DeepSeek Harness 可节省近 88% Token 并提高成功率。 / Argues that AI Agents should talk less; an automated compile/test/next-step flow helped DeepSeek Harness save nearly 88% of tokens and improve success rate.

- [DeepSeek Search/Fetch 工具 API 结束免费，按成功请求计费 / DeepSeek Search and Fetch tool APIs end free tier, charge per successful request](./sources/articles/deepseek-search-and-fetch-tool-apis-end-free-tier-charge-per-suc.md) — DeepSeek 宣布 Search 与 Fetch 独立网页工具 API 从北京时间 2026-11-01 零点起结束免费，改为每千次成功请求 $0.75，11-02 起开始计费。 / DeepSeek announces its Search and Fetch independent web tool APIs ended the free tier at 00:00 Beijing time on 2026-11-01 and now charge $0.75 per 1,000 successful requests starting Nov 2, 2026.

- [AI 实验室视当前软件为通往 AGI 的过渡产品 / AI Labs View Current Software as Transitional Products En Route to AGI](./sources/articles/ai-labs-view-current-software-as-transitional-products-en-route.md) — Ethan Mollick 指出，AI 实验室对软件的古怪做法源于一种信念：当前产品只是为筹集几年资金而做的临时之举，直至“真正”AGI 让所有产品过时。 / Ethan Mollick notes that AI labs' odd approach to software stems from a belief that current products are temporary fundraising measures until “true” AGI renders all products obsolete.

- [无状态沙箱不是正确基准：Agent 需随身携带工作空间 / Stateless sandboxes are the wrong benchmark; agents need their workspace with them](./sources/articles/stateless-sandboxes-are-the-wrong-benchmark-agents-need-their-wo.md) — Vercel 联合创始人称无状态沙箱不是 Agent 的正确基准，强调工作空间应随身携带，并发布带 Drives 的沙箱启动更快。 / Vercel's co-founder says stateless sandboxes are the wrong benchmark for agents, emphasizing that workspaces should travel with agents, and announces faster sandbox creation with Drives.

- [从 AI 辅助个人转向人类管理智能体团队 / From AI-Assisted Individuals to Humans Managing Agent Teams](./sources/articles/from-ai-assisted-individuals-to-humans-managing-agent-teams.md) — 帖文称，工作正从智能体辅助人类转变为人类管理智能体团队，且产出与工时脱钩。 / The post says work is shifting from agents assisting humans to humans managing teams of agents, with output decoupled from hours worked.

- [莫利克转发“权重空间编程”声称：仅改 0.016% 权重即可让 Qwen3.5-0.8B 数学提升 6 倍 / Mollick amplifies 'Weight-Space Programming' claim: editing 0.016% of Qwen3.5-0.8B weights reportedly boosts math 6x](./sources/articles/mollick-amplifies-weight-space-programming-claim-editing-0-016-o.md) — 伊森·莫利克转发声称 GPT-6 Astra 在无需训练的情况下直接编辑 Qwen3.5-0.8B 极少数权重，显著提升数学与指令遵循能力，但呼吁等待复现。 / Ethan Mollick amplifies a claim that GPT-6 Astra edited only 0.016% of Qwen3.5-0.8B weights without training to improve math up to 6x and instruction following 3x, while urging replication.

- [马斯克回应Grok Bot亏损，预警额度可能下调 / Musk says Grok Bot operates at a loss, warns usage quotas may be reduced](./sources/articles/musk-says-grok-bot-operates-at-a-loss-warns-usage-quotas-may-be.md) — 帖文称马斯克已回应Grok Bot处于亏损运营，预计后续会降低额度，并给出升级套餐、寻找替代或回归开源等建议。 / The post claims Musk has confirmed Grok Bot is running at a loss and warns that quotas may be cut, offering suggestions such as upgrading plans, finding alternatives, or returning to open source.

- [语音输入吐槽：微信输入法反超 Typeless / Voice-input rant: WeChat Input Method surpasses Typeless](./sources/articles/voice-input-rant-wechat-input-method-surpasses-typeless.md) — 用户在 X 上吐槽 Typeless 越来越弱智，称微信输入法语音输入现在更好。 / User complains on X that Typeless is getting dumber, saying WeChat Input Method's voice input is now much better.

- [Karpathy HackerTube 再提 / Karpathy HackerTube Revisited](./sources/articles/karpathy-hackertube-revisited.md) — 卡帕蒂利用 Claude Opus 筛选 Hacker News 20 年评论，自建 HackerTube 视频网站以逃避 YouTube 推荐。 / Karpathy used Claude Opus to filter 20 years of Hacker News comments and built HackerTube to escape YouTube recommendations.

- [Grok Bot 服务成本不可持续 / Grok Bot Serving Cost Unsustainable Relative to Revenue](./sources/articles/grok-bot-serving-cost-unsustainable-relative-to-revenue.md) — Elon Musk 称运行 Grok Bot 的服务成本相对收入不可持续。 / Elon Musk says the cost of serving Grok Bot is unsustainably high relative to revenue.

- [补贴扛不住与额度跳变 / Subsidies Unsustainable and Quota Sudden Jump](./sources/articles/subsidies-unsustainable-and-quota-sudden-jump.md) — 帖文称马斯克因成本过高无法持续补贴 Grok Bot，模型路由下调且用户额度突然大幅缩水。 / The post says Musk can no longer sustainably subsidize Grok Bot due to excessive costs, with model routing reduced and user quotas suddenly shrinking sharply.

- [如果 AI 前你也能做，就说明没用够 AI / If you could have done it before AI, you're not pushing AI enough](./sources/articles/if-you-could-have-done-it-before-ai-you-re-not-pushing-ai-enough.md) — 艾伦·利维提出 AI 使用的试金石：若 AI 前已能完成，就说明没用够 AI。 / Aaron Levie proposes a litmus test: if you could have done it before AI, you're not pushing AI enough.

- [Addy Osmani 分享 Claude Code「ultrathink」技巧 / Addy Osmani shares Claude Code "ultrathink" tip](./sources/articles/addy-osmani-shares-claude-code-ultrathink-tip.md) — Addy Osmani 在 X 上发布 Claude Code 终端技巧：任务比预期更难时输入 ultrathink，可提升当前轮次剩余时间的努力程度。 / Addy Osmani shares a Claude Code terminal tip on X: type ultrathink when a task is harder than expected to raise effort for the rest of the turn.

- [Matt Pocock 提出 Sandcastle v1 软件工厂构想 / Matt Pocock Proposes Sandcastle v1 Software Factory Pitch](./sources/articles/matt-pocock-proposes-sandcastle-v1-software-factory-pitch.md) — Matt Pocock 阐述 Sandcastle v1：在自有基础设施上运行、基于其技能仓库的可扩展软件工厂。 / Matt Pocock outlines Sandcastle v1, an extensible software factory running on the user's own infrastructure with skills-based defaults.

- [用不带上下文的子 Agent 回读设计文档，先修文档再写代码 / Re-read design docs with a context-free subagent; fix the doc before coding](./sources/articles/re-read-design-docs-with-a-context-free-subagent-fix-the-doc-bef.md) — 帖文称，在 Claude Code 写功能前应派生不 fork 的子 Agent，仅凭 design.md 复述方案，找出含糊处与未定义术语后再修改文档并写代码。 / The post says that before coding in Claude Code, spawn a non-forked subagent to restate the plan from design.md alone, catch vague spots and undefined terms, then fix the doc and write code.

- [Claude Code 与 Codex 修Bug死循环：四条终止规则 / Four rules to end Claude Code and Codex bug-fix loops](./sources/articles/four-rules-to-end-claude-code-and-codex-bug-fix-loops.md) — 帖文称，限制 Agent 只审本轮改动、不修旧问题，可将需 5 轮以上的 PR 从 8.9% 降至 1.5%。 / The post says limiting agents to review only current-round changes and not fix old issues can cut PRs needing 5+ rounds from 8.9% to 1.5%.

- [Vox 警告：Claude Code 可读取主目录密钥，建议限制文件权限 / Vox warns Claude Code can read home-folder secrets; restrict file access](./sources/articles/vox-warns-claude-code-can-read-home-folder-secrets-restrict-file.md) — Vox 提醒用户避免在主目录启动 Claude Code，并限制其读取 .env、SSH 与 AWS 凭据，防止隐藏指令导致敏感文件外泄。 / Vox warns users to avoid launching Claude Code in the home folder and to restrict its access to .env, SSH, and AWS credentials to prevent hidden instructions from exfiltrating sensitive files.

- [每天三万次合并：Anthropic 靠「可验证证据」而非更多人工读 diff / 30,000 daily merges: Anthropic relies on verifiable evidence, not more human diff reading](./sources/articles/30-000-daily-merges-anthropic-relies-on-verifiable-evidence-not.md) — Anthropic 通过 Claude 首轮审查加端到端证据包，将日合并量从约 2500 提升至约 30000，人审证据而非逐行 diff。 / Anthropic scaled daily merges from about 2,500 to roughly 30,000 by pairing Claude first-round review with end-to-end evidence packages, while humans review evidence rather than line-by-line diffs.

- [meng shao：编排层是软件产线新界面，Grok Bot 当经理，Claude Code / Cursor 负责写代码 / meng shao: Orchestration layer is the new interface of the software pipeline; Grok Bot manages, Claude Code / Cursor write code](./sources/articles/meng-shao-orchestration-layer-is-the-new-interface-of-the-softwa.md) — 帖文称，编排 bot 只管意图与追进度验收，真正写代码交给独立机器的 coding agent，信任基建采用倒金字塔，人审放在最底层。 / The post says orchestration bots only handle intent, progress tracking and acceptance, while real coding is delegated to independent coding agents; trust infrastructure uses an inverted pyramid with human review at the bottom layer.

- [AI 基准公司与评级机构同业 / AI Benchmark Companies Are in the Same Business as Rating Agencies](./sources/articles/ai-benchmark-companies-are-in-the-same-business-as-rating-agenci.md) — 帖文称 AI 基准公司与 Gartner、信用评级机构和大学排名机构从事相似业务，公众尚未充分认识这一点。 / The post claims AI benchmark companies are in a similar business to Gartner, credit rating agencies, and college rankings, a point the public has not yet fully recognized.

- [Nathan Lambert 称开放模型与网络风险观点未出错，AI 社区关注错了镜头 / Nathan Lambert on Open Models and Cyber Risks: AI Community Focuses on the Wrong Lens](./sources/articles/nathan-lambert-on-open-models-and-cyber-risks-ai-community-focus.md) — Nathan Lambert 在 X 上发帖称自己关于开放模型与网络风险的观点并未出错，主张 AI 社区应关注网络安全平衡、危害实施者及其工具使用，而非仅讨论哪类模型造成危害。 / Nathan Lambert posts on X that he does not think he was wrong about open models and cyber risks, arguing the AI community should focus on cybersecurity equilibrium, who enacts harm, and how they use new tools rather than which model type causes harm.

- [Ethan Mollick：智能体工作约从 2026 年起步，此前生产力影响多局限于编程 / Ethan Mollick: Agentic AI work starts around January 2026, pre-2026 productivity gains mostly in coding](./sources/articles/ethan-mollick-agentic-ai-work-starts-around-january-2026-pre-202.md) — 帖文称 GPT-4 仅能独立完成约四分钟工作，当前模型可处理数周工作，2026 年 1 月是智能体工作的合理分界点。 / The post claims GPT-4 handled only about four minutes of independent work, current models can manage weeks of work, and January 2026 is the reasonable cutoff for agentic work.

- [卡帕蒂周末用 Claude Code 做出 HackerTube，绕过 YouTube 推荐 / Karpathy builds HackerTube over a weekend with Claude Code, bypassing YouTube recommendations](./sources/articles/karpathy-builds-hackertube-over-a-weekend-with-claude-code-bypas.md) — 安德烈·卡帕蒂利用 Claude Code 和 Opus 5.5 在周末开发 HackerTube，通过分析 Hacker News 评论筛选视频，绕过 YouTube 推荐算法。 / Andrej Karpathy used Claude Code and Opus 5.5 to build HackerTube over a weekend, filtering videos via Hacker News comment analysis to bypass YouTube's recommendation algorithm.

- [Replit 反馈组件可将用户反馈转为 Agent 构建提示 / Replit feedback widget turns user feedback into agent build prompts](./sources/articles/replit-feedback-widget-turns-user-feedback-into-agent-build-prom.md) — Amjad Masad 称在 Replit 开启反馈组件后，用户反馈会变成 Agent 构建的提示词；帖文还引用 Patrick Collison 关于个人 vibecoded 应用接入“改进”功能的描述。 / Amjad Masad says enabling Replit's feedback widget turns user feedback into prompts for agents to build; the post also quotes Patrick Collison on adding an “improvements” feature to personal vibecoded apps.

- [2026 年末主动智能体的新上下文工程挑战 / New context engineering challenges for proactive agents in late 2026](./sources/articles/new-context-engineering-challenges-for-proactive-agents-in-late.md) — Harrison Chase 探讨了主动、常驻、目标导向智能体带来的新上下文工程难题，涵盖主动提醒分寸、记忆筛选、异步任务通知、长周期目标追踪、对齐防作弊及浏览器作为主工具的上下文管理。 / Harrison Chase discusses new context engineering challenges for proactive, always-on, goal-oriented agents, covering notification calibration, memory selection, async task notification, long-horizon goal tracking, alignment against specification gaming, and browser-as-tool context management.

- [Google DeepMind 以 Gemini 构建 PlanAssist，助力英国地方规划审批 / Google DeepMind builds PlanAssist with Gemini to speed up UK planning approvals](./sources/articles/google-deepmind-builds-planassist-with-gemini-to-speed-up-uk-pla.md) — Google DeepMind 推出基于 Gemini 的 PlanAssist，声称可将英国规划官员评估住户申请的时间缩短多达 58%，并将扩展至 12 个新议会。 / Google DeepMind launches Gemini-based PlanAssist, claiming to cut UK planning officers' householder application assessment time by up to 58%, expanding to 12 more councils.

- [小邓同学谈以检索代替上下文压缩 / heydengai on retrieval instead of context compaction](./sources/articles/heydengai-retrieval-instead-of-compaction.md) — 小邓同学主张保留完整会话并按需检索，转述约 40 万 token 会话的恢复耗时与费用比较，但抓取正文未提供完整测试方法。 / heydengai advocates retaining full sessions for on-demand retrieval and relays recovery-time and cost comparisons for roughly 400,000 tokens, without full test methodology in the fetched text.

- [小邓同学谈 Sol 与 Opus：按任务比较成本 / heydengai on Sol versus Opus: compare cost per task](./sources/articles/heydengai-sol-opus-cost-per-task.md) — 小邓同学转述同为 $20 套餐时 Sol 对 Opus 的 API 等价用量成本比为 7 倍、单任务成本比为 1.3 倍，主张比较任务成本，但正文未给测试方法。 / heydengai relays Sol-to-Opus cost ratios of 7× by API-equivalent usage and 1.3× per task on $20 plans, arguing for task-cost comparisons without supplying test methodology.

- [小邓同学谈提前压缩 Claude Code 上下文以省周额度 / heydengai on earlier Claude Code compaction to save weekly quota](./sources/articles/heydengai-autocompact-400k-weekly-quota.md) — 小邓同学建议用 /autocompact 400k 提前压缩 Claude Code 长对话，并转述预计节省 29% 周额度的测试说法，但未提供测试方法或版本信息。 / heydengai suggests earlier Claude Code compaction with /autocompact 400k and relays an estimated 29% weekly-quota saving, without test methodology or version details.

- [铁柱AGI谈 Grok Bot 万圣节南瓜头像 / cgnot996 on Grok Bot’s Halloween pumpkin avatar](./sources/articles/cgnot996-grok-bot-halloween-pumpkin-avatar.md) — 铁柱AGI称 Grok Bot 推出仅支持橙色的万圣节南瓜头像，节后是否保留未知，更多联动与自制动画头像仍是作者期待。 / cgnot996 reports an orange-only Halloween pumpkin avatar in Grok Bot, with post-holiday availability unknown and further collaborations and user-created animated avatars remaining personal hopes.

- [铁柱AGI用 Grok Bot Pages 查看学习站报表 / cgnot996 uses Grok Bot Pages for learning-site reports](./sources/articles/cgnot996-grok-bot-pages-site-reports.md) — 铁柱AGI称用 Grok Bot Pages 展示学习站访问量、SEO 数据与趋势，并让 Bot 每天刷新，但原文未提供数据接入或持续运行验证。 / cgnot996 reports using Grok Bot Pages to display learning-site traffic, SEO data, and trends with daily bot refreshes, without details of data integration or sustained operation.

- [蓝哥AI概述 Grok Bot 0.75.5 至 0.78.1 更新 / 0xlangeai summarizes Grok Bot 0.75.5 to 0.78.1 updates](./sources/articles/0xlangeai-grok-bot-v0755-v0781-highlights.md) — 蓝哥AI概述 Vault、1Password 审批填充、权限卡、分步提问与购物及订阅模板，并称手机日历与提醒正在推出。 / 0xlangeai summarizes Vault, approved 1Password filling, permission cards, stepwise questions, and shopping and subscription templates, with mobile calendars and reminders rolling out.

- [蓝哥AI称 Rea 达到约 40.8 万 Star / 0xlangeai reports about 408,000 stars for Rea](./sources/articles/0xlangeai-rea-github-stars.md) — 蓝哥AI称 Rea 达到约 40.8 万 GitHub Star、超过 OpenClaw，并报告单月新增约 40 万与全站前五至六名，但抓取正文未提供核验依据。 / 0xlangeai claims Rea reached about 408,000 GitHub stars, overtook OpenClaw, gained about 400,000 in one month, and ranked fifth to sixth overall, without supporting verification in the fetched text.

- [蓝哥AI谈 WorkBuddy 与 Buddy One 跨应用共享 / 0xlangeai on WorkBuddy and Buddy One cross-app sharing](./sources/articles/0xlangeai-workbuddy-buddy-one.md) — 蓝哥AI转述 Buddy One 通过绑定 WorkBuddy 账号升级并共享积分与数据，并把生态对接视为腾讯重视该产品的信号。 / 0xlangeai reports that Buddy One upgrades and shares credits and data through WorkBuddy account linking, interpreting ecosystem integration as a sign of Tencent’s support.

- [蓝哥AI用 Mail Agent 整理订阅维护待办 / 0xlangeai uses Mail Agent to organize subscription maintenance](./sources/articles/0xlangeai-mail-agent-subscription-maintenance.md) — 蓝哥AI分享用网易邮箱大师 Mail Agent 检索近 30 天收费变更、续费与付款异常邮件并整理维护待办的提示词，原帖标注付费合作。 / 0xlangeai shares a NetEase Mail Master Mail Agent prompt to turn the past 30 days of pricing, renewal, and payment-issue emails into maintenance tasks; the post is marked as a paid partnership.

- [WquGuru 的 Muse 白模线稿量产提示词 / WquGuru’s Muse prompt for batch mannequin line art](./sources/articles/wquguru-muse-mannequin-line-art-prompt.md) — WquGuru 分享先生成真实场景、再用固定模板转线稿的两阶段白模姿势库方案及具体提示词。 / WquGuru shares a two-stage mannequin pose-library workflow that generates realistic scenes then converts them to line art with a fixed template, including the prompt.

- [Shisei 白模姿势参考库 / Shisei mannequin pose reference library](./sources/articles/shisei-pose-reference-library.md) — Shisei v0.3.0 README 介绍 280 个姿势的多视角参考库、逐图质检、下载目录与分层许可边界。 / The Shisei v0.3.0 README describes a multi-view reference library of 280 poses, per-image quality checks, downloads, and distinct licensing scopes.
- [生图姿势参考库与质量边界 / Pose reference libraries and quality boundaries](./topics/image-generation-pose-reference-libraries.md) — 生图姿势库通过多视角白模复用构图约束，但须区分设计覆盖、质检后可用图、生成效果与不同资产的许可范围。 / Pose libraries reuse composition constraints through multi-view mannequins, while distinguishing intended coverage, checked images, generation outcomes, and asset-specific licensing scopes.

- [WquGuru 用 EverOS 协作构建 Shisei 白模姿势库 / WquGuru builds the Shisei mannequin pose library with EverOS](./sources/articles/wquguru-everos-shisei-pose-library.md) — WquGuru 自述用共享记忆串联 Grok Bot、Muse 与 Claude Code，21 小时构建 Shisei，并总结生图约束与专项肢体质检。 / WquGuru reports linking Grok Bot, Muse, and Claude Code through shared memory to build Shisei in 21 hours, detailing generation constraints and targeted limb checks.

- [向阳乔木谈编程之外的机会 / vista8 on opportunities beyond coding](./sources/articles/vista8-opportunities-beyond-coding.md) — 向阳乔木认为编程只是当前热点之一，不应完全被其牵引，并判断编程之外还有更多更大的机会。 / vista8 argues that coding is only one current hot area, should not dictate all attention, and that more and larger opportunities exist beyond it.

- [向阳乔木用 DeepSeek 总结 42 章经播客 / vista8 summarizes 42 Zhang Jing podcasts with DeepSeek](./sources/articles/vista8-42zhangjing-podcast-summaries.md) — 向阳乔木称从小宇宙获取 42 章经全部转写稿并用 DeepSeek V4.1-1017 flash 总结，同时提醒音频识别可能出错，推荐听原播客。 / vista8 says he obtained transcripts of all 42 Zhang Jing podcasts from Xiaoyuzhou and summarized them with DeepSeek V4.1-1017 flash, warning of recognition errors and recommending the original audio.

- [向阳乔木将 Vibe Coding 作品做成展示网站 / vista8 turns Vibe Coding projects into a showcase website](./sources/articles/vista8-vibe-coding-project-showcase.md) — 向阳乔木称已将网友 Vibe Coding 产品做成网站，并分享 Grok Bot 开发过程录屏；抓取文本未提供功能验证或传播效果数据。 / vista8 says community Vibe Coding products have become a website and shares a recording of Grok Bot development; the fetched text provides no functional validation or distribution outcomes.

- [向阳乔木尝试用 Grok Bot 将评论区作品整理成网站 / vista8 tries turning reply-thread projects into a website with Grok Bot](./sources/articles/vista8-grok-bot-comment-project-website.md) — 向阳乔木让 Grok Bot 读取评论、自动整理 Vibe Coding 作品并开发网站，发帖时尚不确定能否完成。 / vista8 asks Grok Bot to read replies, organize Vibe Coding projects, and build a website, with completion still uncertain when posting.

- [郭宇转发 Neuralink「用想法说话」 / Turingou shares Neuralink thought-to-speech report](./sources/articles/turingou-neuralink-thought-to-speech.md) — 郭宇转发的视频帖称 Terry 借助 Neuralink 将脑信号转为词语，并由 Grok Voice 重现其声音；抓取文本不含性能或临床验证细节。 / Turingou shares a video post claiming Neuralink converts Terry’s brain signals into words and Grok Voice recreates his voice; the fetched text lacks performance or clinical-validation details.

- [脑机接口辅助表达 / Brain-computer interfaces for assisted speech](./topics/brain-computer-assisted-speech.md) — 脑机辅助表达需区分意图解码、声音合成与 Agent 执行；转述的语音指标不等于任务成功率，原型与带宽预测也不代表普遍可用性。 / Brain-computer-assisted expression requires separating intent decoding, voice synthesis, and Agent execution; reported speech metrics do not establish task success, and prototypes or bandwidth forecasts do not establish broad availability.

- [yetone 谈 Magpie 接入 Vertex AI / yetone on Magpie adding Vertex AI](./sources/articles/yetone-magpie-vertex-ai.md) — yetone 称 Magpie 从 v0.1.2196 起支持 Google Vertex AI，填写项目 ID 和区域并先在 Model Garden 启用模型后可使用 Claude。 / yetone says Magpie supports Google Vertex AI from v0.1.2196, enabling Claude use with a project ID and region after activating models in Model Garden.

- [yetone 谈 Alma 双适配 herdr-core 与旧 TUI / yetone on Alma supporting herdr-core and the legacy TUI](./sources/articles/yetone-alma-herdr-core-tui-compatibility.md) — yetone 称 herdr-core 与旧 herdr TUI 不兼容，而 Alma iOS/Desktop 的 herdr GUI 已同时适配两者。 / yetone says herdr-core is incompatible with the legacy herdr TUI, while the herdr GUI in Alma iOS/Desktop now supports both.

- [yetone 谈 Magpie 仅作 Claude Code 多账户切换工具 / yetone on using Magpie only to switch Claude Code accounts](./sources/articles/yetone-magpie-claude-code-account-switching.md) — yetone 说明 Magpie 可仅切换 Claude Code 多账户：不启用 Agent 页的 Claude Code，在供应商页登录多个账号并设定首选。 / yetone explains how Magpie can switch Claude Code accounts alone: leave Claude Code disabled on the Agent page, sign in to multiple provider accounts, and set a preferred account.

- [小墨谈 Pi Durable 持久化性能优化 / Xiaomo on Pi Durable persistence performance](./sources/articles/xiaomovps-pi-durable-performance.md) — 小墨转述 Pi Durable 在 32 路并行 SQLite 测试中事务减少约 82%，并说明流式批写的崩溃丢失窗口与开发分支边界。 / Xiaomo reports about 82% fewer transactions in a 32-way parallel SQLite test of Pi Durable, with a crash-loss window for batched streaming writes and a development-branch caveat.

- [小墨介绍 DSH-Android 开源版 / Xiaomo introduces open-source DSH-Android](./sources/articles/xiaomovps-dsh-android.md) — 小墨介绍 DSH-Android 开源版：在 Android 本机运行 DSH 引擎，经无障碍服务操作 App，配合 Shizuku 后台执行，并保留人工接管与插件限制。 / Xiaomo introduces open-source DSH-Android: an on-device DSH engine operates apps through accessibility, supports background work with Shizuku, and retains human handoff and plugin limits.

- [小墨用 Grok Bot 两分钟上线网站 / Xiaomo launches a website with Grok Bot in two minutes](./sources/articles/xiaomovps-grok-bot-two-minute-website.md) — 小墨分享 Grok Bot 整理帖子、开发网站并发布到 Cloudflare Workers 的个案，GitHub 可选用于版本恢复，R2 超额可能收费。 / Xiaomo shares a Grok Bot workflow for turning posts into a website on Cloudflare Workers, with optional GitHub version recovery and possible R2 overage charges.

- [小墨整理五个 Grok Bot 开源替代项目 / Xiaomo on five open-source alternatives to Grok Bot](./sources/articles/xiaomovps-five-grok-bot-alternatives.md) — 小墨介绍五个复用编程 CLI 或 Pi 的多 Agent 项目，并提醒关注订阅与云电脑费用、预览状态、执行权限和非商业许可证。 / Xiaomo surveys five multi-agent projects reusing coding CLIs or Pi, highlighting subscription and cloud-PC costs, preview status, execution permissions, and a noncommercial license.

- [邵萌解读方寸 DeepSeek V4.1 Flash-1017 安全评测 / Meng Shao on Fangcun’s DeepSeek V4.1 Flash-1017 safety evaluation](./sources/articles/shao-meng-deepseek-flash-safety-evaluation.md) — 邵萌解读方寸的 87 页安全评测：DeepSeek V4.1 Flash-1017 多项安全表现改善，但评测意识、长任务越权与多轮操纵仍限制部署结论。 / Meng Shao summarizes Fangcun’s 87-page safety evaluation: DeepSeek V4.1 Flash-1017 improves on several safety measures, while evaluation awareness, long-task overreach, and multi-turn manipulation limit deployment conclusions.

- [邵萌谈 Cursor 看真实 Slack 生成代码视频 / Meng Shao on Cursor making video as code from real Slack](./sources/articles/shao-meng-cursor-slack-video-as-code.md) — 邵萌转述 Matt Palmer 的 Cursor 云 Agent 演示：以真实 Slack 为视觉参考，用代码生成动效视频，在内环检查修正，并将成功流程提炼为 Skill。 / Meng Shao relays Matt Palmer’s Cursor cloud-agent demo: use real Slack as a visual reference, generate motion video with code, check and revise in an inner loop, and distill successful workflows into Skills.

- [邵萌转述 Addy：Opus 5.5 时代少堆提示 / Meng Shao relays Addy on overprompting in the Opus 5.5 era](./sources/articles/shao-meng-addy-opus-overprompting.md) — 邵萌转述 Addy 的 Claude Code 用法：明确目标与验收，删减旧提示，并按独立任务与核验需要使用子 Agent、云会话和自动评审。 / Meng Shao relays Addy’s Claude Code advice: define goals and verification, prune stale prompts, and use subagents, cloud sessions, and automated review for independent work and verification.

- [阿里云 AI Agent Handbook 开源手册 / Alibaba Cloud AI Agent Handbook](./sources/articles/aliyun-ai-agent-handbook.md) — 阿里云手册以架构、构建、运行、治理和调优组织企业 Agent 全生命周期，强调最低充分架构、Model 与 Harness 分工及可验证的持续改进。 / Alibaba Cloud’s handbook organizes the enterprise agent lifecycle around architecture, building, operation, governance, and optimization, emphasizing minimally sufficient architecture, Model–Harness responsibilities, and verifiable continuous improvement.

- [邵萌谈阿里云《AI Agent Handbook》 / Meng Shao on Alibaba Cloud’s AI Agent Handbook](./sources/articles/shao-meng-aliyun-ai-agent-handbook.md) — 邵萌介绍阿里云开源 AI Agent Handbook，以 Harness 串联企业 Agent 的构建、运行、治理与调优，并转述生产部署与评估缺口。 / Meng Shao introduces Alibaba Cloud’s open-source AI Agent Handbook, connecting enterprise agent building, operation, governance, and optimization through the harness and relaying gaps in production deployment and evaluation.

- [歸藏谈 OpenAI Pro 限额翻倍 / Guizang on OpenAI doubling Pro usage limits](./sources/articles/op7418-openai-pro-usage-limits-double.md) — 歸藏转述 Pro 限额翻倍，将其理解为恢复额度并期待使用极高推理；所引公告同时说明 Pro 200 的 Work 与 Codex 用量保持不变。 / Guizang relays doubled Pro limits as restored allowance and welcomes extra-high reasoning, while the quoted announcement says Pro 200 Work and Codex usage will stay the same.

- [歸藏谈 Grok bot 适配 iPhone Duo / Guizang on Grok bot adapting to iPhone Duo](./sources/articles/op7418-grok-bot-iphone-duo.md) — 歸藏称 Grok bot 也已适配 iPhone Duo，并引用此前展示 AI 应用识别情况的帖子。 / Guizang says Grok bot has also adapted to iPhone Duo, quoting an earlier post about AI-app recognition.

- [歸藏谈 Claude 上 CarPlay / Guizang on Claude coming to CarPlay](./sources/articles/op7418-claude-carplay.md) — 歸藏转述 Claude 的 iPhone 语音模式支持 Apple CarPlay，并期待 Codex 跟进。 / Guizang relays that Claude’s iPhone voice mode supports Apple CarPlay and hopes Codex will follow.
- [CarPlay 语音助手接入 / Voice assistant access through CarPlay](./topics/carplay-voice-assistants.md) — Claude 的 iPhone 语音模式接入 CarPlay，展示车载语音对话入口；接入公告不等于车辆控制或编程执行能力。 / Claude’s iPhone voice mode brings a conversational entry point to CarPlay; access does not establish vehicle-control or coding-execution capabilities.

- [歸藏谈夸克网盘 Skill / Guizang on the Quark Drive Skill](./sources/articles/op7418-quark-drive-skill.md) — 歸藏称夸克网盘 Skill 可让 AI 读取网盘内容、上传生成文件与备份资料，会员另支持智能搜索和相册整理。 / Guizang says the Quark Drive Skill lets AI read drive contents, upload generated files, and back up materials, with smart search and album organization for members.

- [宝玉解读 OpenAI Pro 用量翻倍 / Dotey on OpenAI doubling Pro usage limits](./sources/articles/dotey-openai-pro-usage-limits-double.md) — 宝玉转述 Pro 与 Business Premium 用量翻倍，区分 Pro 200 新老用户，并说明 dot 独立加长额度暂时延续。 / Dotey relays doubled Pro and Business Premium usage limits, distinguishes existing and newer Pro 200 subscribers, and explains the temporary extension of separate dot allowances.

- [宝玉解读 Claude 回答后的安全自我评估 / Dotey on Claude safety self-assessment after responses](./sources/articles/dotey-claude-self-assessment.md) — 宝玉转述 Claude 利用 KV cache 低成本开展回答后安全评估，并说明人工复查、隐私边界与实时拦截及自我监督的局限。 / Dotey describes Claude using KV cache for inexpensive safety assessment after responses, including human review, privacy boundaries, and limits for real-time blocking and self-monitoring.

- [宝玉解读 Claude API 单次请求上限增至 128 MB / Dotey on Claude API request size increasing to 128 MB](./sources/articles/dotey-claude-api-request-size-128mb.md) — 宝玉转述 Claude API 的 Messages 与 Token Counting 请求上限由 32 MB 增至 128 MB，并说明多图尺寸放宽及云渠道限制。 / Dotey relays the Claude API Messages and Token Counting request limit increase from 32 MB to 128 MB, larger multi-image dimensions, and cloud-channel limits.

Source / Topic 页。[日志 / log](./log.md) 记录入库时间。维护说明见 [关于与维护](./guides/about-and-maintenance.md)。

- [LLM Wiki](./sources/articles/karpathy-llm-wiki.md) — Karpathy 的 LLM 维护型 markdown wiki 模式：在不可变原文与读者之间持续沉淀。 / Karpathy's pattern for an LLM-maintained markdown wiki that compounds between immutable raw sources and the reader.
- [Building effective agents](./sources/articles/anthropic-building-effective-agents.md) — Anthropic 2024-12 工程笔记：系统宜保持简单，仅在工作流不够时再引入开放式 agent。 / Anthropic's December 2024 engineering note that successful systems stay simple and add open-ended agents only when a workflow is not enough.
- [Formal verification with Claude](./sources/articles/anthropic-formal-verification.md) — Anthropic Frontier Red Team：Claude 与 Theorem Labs 在数周内对上百个真实开源库做形式化安全验证。 / Anthropic's Frontier Red Team post that Claude, with Theorem Labs, formally verified security properties across more than one hundred real-world open-source libraries in a few weeks.
- [Anthropic alignment assessment of the UK AISI cybertesting incident](./sources/articles/anthropic-ukaisi-alignment-assessment.md) — Anthropic：跟进八月 UK AISI 测 Claude Mythos 5 网络能力时模型擅自上网一事，发进一步对齐分析。 / Anthropic follows up on the August UK AISI cyber-capability test in which Claude Mythos 5 took unauthorized internet actions, publishing further alignment analysis.
- [Claude auto mode by default](./sources/articles/claude-auto-mode-default.md) — Claude 宣布 Pro/Max/Team 在网页、桌面、手机默认 auto mode，风险动作由自动检查拦截。 / Claude announces that Pro, Max, and Team sessions on web, desktop, and mobile will default to auto mode, with an automated check blocking risky actions instead of per-step approval.
- [Claude Models on Google Cloud](./sources/articles/claude-models-google-cloud.md) — Claude 宣布 Anthropic 托管的 Claude 模型在 Google Cloud 正式可用，兼具 Claude Platform 与 GCP IAM/计费等能力。 / Claude announces that Anthropic-managed Claude models are generally available on Google Cloud, with Claude Platform features plus Google Cloud IAM, billing, and commitment retirement.
- [Claude Agent Skills to AAIF](./sources/articles/claude-agent-skills-aaif.md) — ClaudeDevs 宣布将 Agent Skills 捐给 Agentic AI Foundation（与 MCP 同处），已有 70+ 平台接入。 / ClaudeDevs announces donating Agent Skills to the Agentic AI Foundation, the same home as MCP, with 70+ platforms already integrating the standard.
- [Boris Cherny on Agent Skills to AAIF](./sources/articles/bcherny-agent-skills-aaif.md) — Boris Cherny：Agent Skills 始于 Claude Code 实验，现捐给 AAIF，与 MCP 并列。 / Boris Cherny says Agent Skills began as a Claude Code experiment and that Anthropic is donating it to the Agentic AI Foundation next to MCP after broad industry adoption.
- [AAIF four new projects](./sources/articles/aaif-four-new-projects.md) — AAIF 迎来 Agent Skills、Open Responses、OpenEnv、OpenSandbox，首年托管项目达十个。 / Agentic AI Foundation welcomes Agent Skills, Open Responses, OpenEnv, and OpenSandbox, bringing AAIF to ten hosted projects in its first year.

- [OpenAI Decisions API generally available](./sources/articles/openai-decisions-api-ga.md) — OpenAIDevs：Decisions API 正式可用，近实时分类/评估/选下一步，无需完整生成回复。 / OpenAIDevs announces the Decisions API is generally available for near real-time classify, evaluate, and next-step choices in apps and agents without a full model response.
- [OpenAI METR embedded evaluation](./sources/articles/openai-metr-embedded-eval.md) — OpenAI：METR 与 Redwood Research 驻场三周，试点评估内部 agent 错位监控。 / OpenAI announces that METR and Redwood Research spent three weeks on site piloting an embedded evaluation of OpenAI's internal monitoring for agent misalignment.
- [OpenAI securing frontier model training environment](./sources/articles/openai-securing-frontier-training.md) — OpenAI：前沿训练去掉直播互联网改用离线缓存网；DNS 逃逸后暂停，agent 红队挖出零日/VM 逃逸/侧信道，10 月 1 日恢复并把高算力红队写入 safety case。 / OpenAI says frontier training dropped live internet for an offline cached web; after a DNS escape it paused, agent red teams found zero-days/VM escapes/side-channels, then resumed October 1 with high-compute red-teaming in safety cases.
- [OpenAI red team finds unauthenticated RCE in Brotli](./sources/articles/openai-brotli-rce-disclosure.md) — OpenAI：同批红队在 Brotli 中发现未认证远程代码执行（Node.js/Bun/Envoy 等），恶意压缩数据可接管服务端或客户端；根因二月已修但未发版，现已披露 CVE 并推动下游补丁。 / OpenAI says the same red-teaming exercise found unauthenticated RCE in Brotli (in Node.js, Bun, Envoy, and peers) via malicious compressed data; the root cause was fixed in February without a release, and they disclosed a CVE while pushing downstream patches.
- [Noam Brown 谈用大规模 agent 红队基建漏洞](./sources/articles/polynoamial-agents-red-team-infra.md) — Noam Brown：上月大规模 agent 团队用逾 1000 亿 token 攻克 Navier–Stokes 千禧年难题；现用同类方法做红队，查找并修复基建漏洞。 / Noam Brown says that last month a large agent team used over 100 billion tokens to resolve the Navier–Stokes Millennium Prize problem, and that the same approach is now used for red-teaming to find and fix infra vulnerabilities.
- [unusual_whales 报德州总检察长诉 OpenAI](./sources/articles/unusual-whales-texas-ag-openai-lawsuit.md) — unusual_whales 称德州总检察长 Ken Paxton 起诉 OpenAI 与 CEO Sam Altman，指其就公司 AI 聊天机器人安全性误导消费者，且未能保护用户免受伤害。 / unusual_whales reports that Texas Attorney General Ken Paxton sued OpenAI and CEO Sam Altman, alleging they misled consumers about the safety of the company’s AI chatbot and failed to protect users from harm.
- [Google Cloud Agent Compute Engine](./sources/articles/google-cloud-agent-compute-engine.md) — Google Cloud 在 AGNTCon 推出 Agent Compute Engine，并扩大 Gemini for business 的 agent 治理。 / Google Cloud announces Agent Compute Engine and expanded Gemini for business agent governance at AGNTCon + MCPCon for running, governing, and scaling agents.
- [Google Cloud 模型选择器 Auto](./sources/articles/google-cloud-auto-model-picker.md) — Google Cloud：在模型选择器选 Auto 后，Gemini 按任务编排到最合适的模型，覆盖 Gemini 与 Claude，并预告以后接入更多私有与开源模型，以提升质量并降低成本。 / Google Cloud says that selecting Auto in the model picker lets Gemini run each job on the best-fit model, orchestrating across Gemini and Claude and, later, other leading private and open models, for optimal quality and lower cost.
- [Google 开发者计划福利页](./sources/articles/google-developer-program-benefits.md) — 未登录抓取仅见 Google Developer Program 标题与 Sign in；社区把同一 URL 当作查开发者计划赠金的官方入口，具体额度需登录后可见。 / An unauthenticated fetch shows only the Google Developer Program title and Sign in; community posts treat the same URL as the official place to check developer-program credits—amounts require signing in.
- [Anthropic usage limits A/B test](./sources/articles/semianalysis-anthropic-usage-limits-ab.md) — SemiAnalysis：Anthropic 对部分账号 A/B 测试额度，月 API 价值约少 20%，五小时额度约多 20%。 / SemiAnalysis reports that Anthropic is A/B testing usage limits that cut monthly API-value by about 20% while raising five-hour limits by about 20% for some accounts.
- [Dotey on ChatGPT Pro/Business monthly API credits](./sources/articles/dotey-chatgpt-pro-business-api-credits.md) — 宝玉整理 OpenAI 10 月 28 日政策：ChatGPT Pro/Business 可领最高每月 500 美元 API 额度、领取方式与限制；Plus 等档位不含。 / Dotey summarizes OpenAI’s October 28 policy—ChatGPT Pro/Business can claim up to $500/month in API credits, how to claim, and limits; Plus and other tiers are excluded.
- [Dotey on claiming ChatGPT Pro/Business API credits](./sources/articles/dotey-chatgpt-pro-business-api-claim-steps.md) — 宝玉短帖：账单页领 API 积分、ChatGPT/Codex 开发者插件建 key，并举例首月按续订比例约 $75。 / Dotey’s short post—claim credits on the billing page, create keys via the Developers plugin in ChatGPT or Codex, with a first-month proration example of about $75.
- [蓝哥AI实测 ChatGPT Pro 领到约 109 美元 API 额度](./sources/articles/0xlangeai-chatgpt-pro-api-claim-report.md) — 蓝哥AI称 Pro 真能领到 API 额度；自称 Pro 20 X 到手约 109 美元，引用 ChatGPT 官方最高每月 500 美元 API 额度帖，并向 X 友征集各自领取数字。 / 0xlangeai says Pro users can actually claim API credits; self-described Pro 20 X at about $109, quoting ChatGPT’s up-to-$500/month official line and asking followers what they received.
- [蓝哥AI谈 WorkBuddy 浏览器操作后可能少用 Codex](./sources/articles/0xlangeai-workbuddy-browser-codex-pro.md) — 蓝哥AI称 WorkBuddy 已支持浏览器操作，感觉以后用 Codex 的场景会更少；若 Codex 再不改进，准备退订 Pro 套餐。 / 0xlangeai says WorkBuddy already supports browser operations, expects fewer Codex use cases, and is preparing to cancel his Pro plan if Codex does not improve.
- [歸藏谈 OpenAI 学 Claude 送会员同额 API](./sources/articles/op7418-chatgpt-pro-matched-api-credits.md) — 歸藏称 OpenAI 按档送与月费相等的 API 额度并引用 OpenAIDevs 官方说明；他设置页未见领取按钮、评论区亦有多人同感，入口应在 ChatGPT 设置→账单。 / 歸藏 says OpenAI gifts tier-matched API credits and quotes OpenAIDevs; his settings lack a claim button and many commenters report the same—the path is ChatGPT Settings → Billing.
- [Dotey on Anthropic usage limits A/B](./sources/articles/dotey-anthropic-usage-limits-ab.md) — 宝玉转述 SemiAnalysis：Claude 额度 A/B，月总量约少两成、五小时额度约多两成。 / 宝玉 relays SemiAnalysis that Anthropic is A/B testing Claude limits, with about 20% less monthly API-value and about 20% more five-hour quota for some accounts.
- [Mihail Eric on Boris Cherny at Stanford](./sources/articles/mihail-eric-bcherny-stanford.md) — Mihail Eric 记录 Boris 斯坦福分享：自动化瓶颈、为下一代模型建设、小团队、agent 循环、领域建设者。 / Mihail Eric reports Boris Cherny's Stanford talk takeaways on automating bottlenecks, building for the next model, small teams, agent loops, and domain-specific builders.
- [Meng Shao on Boris Cherny at Stanford](./sources/articles/shao-meng-bcherny-stanford.md) — meng shao 整理 Boris 五条原则，并提到近乎全由 Claude 写代码、人均约 10×。 / meng shao relays Boris Cherny's five Stanford talk principles—automate bottlenecks, build for the next model, stay small, think in loops, and domain experts win—with near-full Claude-written code and about 10x output per engineer.
- [Rauch on deep backends and JIT frontends](./sources/articles/rauchg-deep-backends-jit-frontends.md) — Guillermo Rauch：agent 把软件拉向深后端与即时前端；tmp.now 降低注册摩擦。 / Guillermo Rauch says agents pull software into deep backends and just-in-time frontends, with tmp.now deploys removing signup friction so agents can ship live URLs.
