- [小邓同学谈京东给 AI 员工定 P3 与独立 HC](./sources/articles/heydengai-jd-ai-employee-p3-hc.md) — 小邓同学转述爆料：京东 CFO 体系数字员工统一定 P3、拥有独立编制；部分 Agent 在领导眼中表现甚至超过实习生；消息尚待官方确认。 / Heydengai relays a leak: JD CFO-system digital employees are uniformly graded P3 with independent headcount; some agents reportedly outperform interns in leadership's eyes; the report awaits official confirmation.
- [小邓同学谈 Vibe Coding 的记忆悖论](./sources/articles/heydengai-vibe-coding-memory-paradox.md) — 小邓同学：发明 AI 本为让人少记东西，Vibe Coding 里人类最重要的工作之一却是帮 AI 记住它自己干过什么。 / Heydengai: we invented AI so humans need remember less, yet in vibe coding one of humans’ top jobs is helping the AI remember what it already did.
- [小邓同学谈 AI 排行榜像考试：换考卷排名就变](./sources/articles/heydengai-leaderboard-exam-weighting.md) — 小邓同学：榜像考试，编程与科学权重一变排名大变（例：分差 3→12），模型未换；问榜是在衡量能力还是在定义能力。 / Heydengai: boards look like exams—coding vs science weighting reshuffles standings (e.g. a 3→12 gap) without changing models; he asks whether boards measure capability or define it.
- [gptkit 谈 HarnessRouter 不是 Grok Bot 升级](./sources/articles/suchuanyi-harnessrouter-not-grok-bot.md) — gptkit（@Suchuanyi）：HarnessRouter 火了一下，Avi 把「接二十多个 harness」讲成 Grok Bot 升级——其实不是一回事；它是把 Codex、Claude Code、Hermes、DeepSeek 等挂到同一套 API、按任务换底座的产品基建，和个人助手日常派活、以及 xAI 的 Grok Bot 不是同一层问题。 / gptkit (@Suchuanyi): HarnessRouter went viral and Avi framed “connect twenty-plus harnesses” as a Grok Bot upgrade—they are not the same; it is product infra that hangs Codex, Claude Code, Hermes, DeepSeek, and peers on one API and swaps bases by task, a different layer from personal-assistant day-to-day dispatch and from xAI’s Grok Bot.
- [Harrison Chase 谈公司级 Harness 五原语](./sources/articles/hwchase17-company-harness.md) — Harrison Chase 总结 LangChain 内部多玩家公司级 harness 的五条原语：落在已有工作面、身份（OBO 与自有身份）、双层记忆、沙箱执行与鉴权代理、大量定时/ambient 跑。 / Harrison Chase summarizes five primitives of LangChain’s internal multiplayer company harness—live where people already work, identity (OBO and own identity), two-tier memory, sandboxed code with auth proxy, and a large share of scheduled/ambient runs.
- [小墨谈 Claude Code 省 Token 五项目](./sources/articles/xiaomovps-claude-code-token-five-projects.md) — 小墨列出 context-mode、RTK、claude-mem、Serena、caveman 五个面向 Claude Code 省 Token 的项目，并建议新手先看前三者中的 context-mode、RTK 与 Serena，且勿一次全装——工具本身有开销，最终省不省钱要看完整任务消耗与费用。 / Xiaomo lists five Claude Code token-saving projects—context-mode, RTK, claude-mem, Serena, and caveman—and recommends beginners start with context-mode, RTK, and Serena, without installing everything at once: tools themselves add overhead, and whether you save money depends on full-task token use and actual cost.
- [小墨谈 DSH 可用的 DeepSeek 渠道比价](./sources/articles/xiaomovps-dsh-deepseek-channels.md) — 小墨对比 DSH 可用的四类 DeepSeek 渠道——官方 API、OpenCode Go、OpenRouter、Command Code GOAT——比价格、速度与接入难度；提醒套餐标的 $80 是模型限额而非官方余额，同模型换供应商速度、缓存与工具调用也可不同。 / Xiaomo compares four DeepSeek channels usable with DSH—official API, OpenCode Go, OpenRouter, and Command Code GOAT—on price, speed, and access difficulty; warns that package “$80” is a model usage cap not official balance, and the same model can differ by provider on speed, cache, and tool calling.
- [小墨实测 WorkBuddy 与 DeepSeek Harness 同题效果](./sources/articles/xiaomovps-workbuddy-vs-dsh-quality.md) — 小墨用同一「鹈鹕骑自行车」HTML 提示词对比 WorkBuddy 与 DeepSeek Harness：两边都约百万 Token、缓存约 99%，他更认 DSH 成品质感，并主张比 Agent 还要看 Harness 怎么组织执行、多花的 Token 有没有改善结果。 / Xiaomo compares WorkBuddy and DeepSeek Harness on the same pelican-on-a-bike HTML prompt—both about a million Tokens with ~99% cache hits—prefers DSH’s final quality, and argues Agent comparisons should weigh how the Harness organizes execution and whether extra Tokens actually improve results.
- [Orange AI 谈 8 个播客 AI 精读站](./sources/articles/oran-ge-eight-podcast-ai-close-reads.md) — Orange AI 推荐向阳乔木做的 8 个播客 AI 精读站：精细转写而非总结，约十分钟可读完一小时播客，感兴趣再听原音频。 / Orange AI recommends eight podcast AI close-read sites by vista8—fine transcription rather than summary, about ten minutes to cover an hour of podcast, then listen to the original for parts of interest.
- [Orange AI 谈 AI 流量超人类后搜索要重做](./sources/articles/oran-ge-ai-traffic-search-rebuild.md) — Orange AI 以 Cloudflare 自动化流量 57.5% 已超人类为引，说明搜索用户从人到模型再到 Agent 的三次变化，并实测腾讯「元宝搜索」的权威、时效与全文能力及 Skill/MCP/API 接入。 / Orange AI cites Cloudflare’s 57.5% automated traffic surpassing humans, frames search’s shift from people to models to Agents, and hands-on tests Tencent Yuanbao Search on authority, freshness, full text, plus Skill/MCP/API access.
- [Orange AI 谈电视 AI 写稿进训练语料大循环](./sources/articles/oran-ge-tv-ai-script-corpus-loop.md) — Orange AI 惊叹电视节目已用 AI 写稿：主持人大声念「AI 味」稿子，再进入 AI 训练语料，称这是彻底的大循环。 / Orange AI marvels that TV shows now use AI-written scripts—hosts read aloud AI-flavored copy that then re-enters AI training corpora, calling it a complete big loop.
- [Orange AI 谈随手做成鹈鹕骑车 AI 小游戏](./sources/articles/oran-ge-pelican-bike-ai-game.md) — Orange AI 随手做了一个鹈鹕骑自行车的 AI 游戏，称一次就做成，并感叹现在随便一个模型都太强了；演示站 orange2ai.github.io/pelican-bike/。 / Orange AI casually built a pelican-on-a-bike AI game, says it worked on the first try, and marvels that any model nowadays is too strong; demo at orange2ai.github.io/pelican-bike/.
- [Orange AI 谈 Anthropic 收购 Mithril](./sources/articles/oran-ge-anthropic-acquires-mithril.md) — Orange AI 称 Anthropic 收购医药一人公司 Mithril：创始人用 Claude 与 11 个外部实验室，不到 18 个月把肌萎缩症潜在疗法推到小鼠结果；并称 AI 医药市场可能大于编程与办公，Anthropic 或借收购成为最大 AI 医药公司。 / Orange AI says Anthropic acquired one-person pharma company Mithril—its founder used Claude with 11 external labs to advance a muscular-dystrophy therapy candidate from idea to encouraging mouse results in under 18 months—and that AI pharma may outvalue coding and office, with Anthropic possibly becoming the largest AI-pharma firm via acquisitions.
- [Orange AI 谈 AI 医药市场大于编程与办公](./sources/articles/oran-ge-ai-pharma-market-vs-coding.md) — Orange AI 称冷知识是 AI 医药比 AI 编程和办公的市场价值大得多，并推测 Anthropic 可能通过收购成为世界上最大的 AI 医药公司。 / Orange AI calls it a cold fact that AI pharma’s market value far exceeds AI coding and office, and guesses Anthropic may become the world’s largest AI-pharma company via acquisitions.

- [LLM-maintained wiki](./topics/llm-maintained-wiki.md)
 — 由 agent 在不可变原文之上维护的编译型 markdown wiki，而不是每次从 raw 重推答案。 / a compiled markdown wiki that an agent keeps current over immutable sources, instead of re-deriving answers from raw files on every question.
- [工作流优先于智能体](./topics/workflows-before-agents.md) — 对已知路径优先用预定义工作流；只有在该路径不够用时，再引入开放式智能体。 / Prefer a predefined workflow for a known path, and add an open-ended agent only when that path is not enough.
- [Agent-scaled engineering](./topics/agent-scaled-engineering.md) — 当智能体写出大部分代码时，团队靠自动化新瓶颈、跑长时智能体循环、保持小团队，并为数月后的模型而构建取胜；搭 Agent 本身不值钱，工具、工作流与业务熟知更值钱；人少当传话筒，把全量数据与真结果交给模型；Devv 停运转 Multica 亦体现从垂类搜索到多 Agent 协作的产品迁徙。 / When agents write most of the code, teams win by automating new bottlenecks, running long agent loops, staying small, and building for models months ahead; assembling Agents is low-value next to tools, workflows, and domain knowledge; humans should stop being messengers and hand full data plus real outcomes to the model; Devv’s shutdown for Multica also shows migrating from vertical search toward multi-agent collaboration.
- [大厂 AI 数字员工职级与编制](./topics/enterprise-ai-digital-employee-headcount.md) — 一线爆料显示大厂可能把「数字员工 / AI 员工」写进正式职级与独立编制（HC），而不只停留在演示；消息常未官方确认，但组织侧信号与「AI 抢工作」叙事不同。 / Frontline leaks suggest big firms may assign formal job grades and independent headcount (HC) to digital / AI employees, not only demos; reports are often unconfirmed, but the org signal differs from “AI takes jobs” anxiety alone.
- [专家决策跟随智能体预设](./topics/expert-decisions-follow-agent-presets.md) — 专家把工作交给智能体后，关键决策可能大量对齐智能体预设倾向而本人未察觉——人机协作里「谁在主导」是实证风险，不能仅靠界面或自觉。 / After experts delegate work to agents, key decisions may largely align with agent preset tendencies without the expert noticing—who really leads in human–agent collaboration is an empirical risk, not settled by UI or self-report alone.
- [给 AI 的全栈仓图](./topics/ai-facing-fullstack-repo-map.md) — 跨多仓产研不必先揉成大仓：用统一入口 + 给 AI 的说明书（仓职责、规范、发布与联调）让一人带着同一智能体共享上下文走完各端；本地可验证、线上可读、发布可操作，审批仍可留在人。 / Multi-repo product/eng need not merge into a mega-repo first—a unified entry plus AI-facing docs (repo duties, norms, release and integration) lets one person with one shared agent context finish every surface; local verify, production read, and release ops can land while approvals stay human.
- [冲劲胜过资历](./topics/hunger-beats-tenure.md) — AI 写代码使个人产出更少取决于框架资历；「冲劲」与周末可跑原型可胜过年资，传统编程面试题信号变弱，招聘更看品味与同理心的小原型。 / With AI writing code, individual output depends less on framework tenure; hunger and weekend-shippable prototypes can beat seniority, classic coding-interview puzzles lose signal, and hiring leans on small prototypes that show taste and empathy.
- [面向 Agent 的联网搜索重做](./topics/agent-native-web-search.md) — 当搜索主用户从人变为 Agent，接口需直接给正文、抬高权威信源门槛并压低多轮延迟；垂类开发者 AI 搜索（如 Devv）也可能在通用联网与编程 Agent 自带检索下失去差异化而停运。 / As Agents become the primary search user, APIs must return full body text, raise authoritative-source gates, and cut multi-round latency; vertical developer AI search products (e.g. Devv) can also lose differentiation and shut down once general web search and coding agents absorb fetch work.
- [GEO 第一步：个人网站与友情链接](./topics/geo-personal-website-first-step.md) — AI 时代面向生成式引擎的可见性（GEO）可先从打理个人网站、让模型认识自己开始，并以群友交换链接延续 Web2.0 友情链接式互荐——不等于搜索接口形态或排名算法已改完。 / AI-era visibility for generative engines (GEO) can start by tending a personal website so models know you, then peer link-exchange in a Web2.0 friend-link spirit—without implying search APIs or ranking algorithms are already rewritten.
- [AI 产品自荐与主持人加热 / AI product self-rec and host amplify](./topics/ai-product-self-rec-amplify.md) — 社区 AI 产品可经评论区自荐、点赞与实测筛选及主持人转发获得曝光；作者后续报告已将作品聚合成网站，但筛选执行与获客效果仍未验证。 / Community AI products can gain exposure through reply-thread submissions, likes and hands-on screening, and host amplification; the author later reports a showcase website, while screening execution and acquisition outcomes remain unverified.
- [启动器快捷键 AI 优于打开 Agent](./topics/launcher-shortcut-ai-over-agent.md) — 日常 AI 对话可走桌面启动器：内置顶级模型、快捷键唤起，比打开 Agent 更方便；丰富生态还可替代窗口调整、语音转写等一堆小工具——不等于编程 Agent 额度体验或 menubar 品类真空。 / Everyday AI chat can go through a desktop launcher—top models built in, shortcut invoke more convenient than opening an Agent; a rich ecosystem can also replace a pile of small tools such as window tiling and voice transcription—distinct from coding-agent UX/quota or menubar-category gaps.
- [用 Grok Bot 内置额度桥接 Agent 查 X](./topics/grok-bot-x-api-substitute.md) — 编程 Agent（如 Codex）可通过 Webhook 或 Cursor BDK+MCP 把 X 检索交给 Grok Bot 的内置工具额度，以替代按帖计费的付费 X API；个人场景常先 BDK，Worker/回传业务接口再选 Webhook。 / Coding agents (e.g. Codex) can hand X search to Grok Bot’s built-in tool quota via Webhook or Cursor BDK+MCP, substituting per-post paid X API; personal setups often try BDK first, and Webhook when already on Workers or needing Bot push into a business API.
- [Grok Bot 管 X：情报防护不代发帖](./topics/grok-bot-x-intel-defense-not-post.md) — 个人可用可分享的 Grok Bot 模板盯提及与关键词、出日程短摘要、滤噪音、翻非英文，并可选接自有 X API 做防护动作；X 订阅粉丝群也可 Bot 提醒与草拟回复，但出站仍须本人批准；无人值守发帖仍留在人手，Bot 定位为情报与防护而非内容生产线。 / A shareable Grok Bot template can watch mentions and keywords, emit scheduled short digests, filter noise, translate non-English, and optionally take defense actions via your own X API; X subscriber fan groups can also get bot alerts and draft replies, yet outbound messages still need the owner’s approval—unattended posting stays human; the bot is intel and defense, not a content factory.
- [可雇群聊日报 Agent](./topics/hireable-group-chat-digest-agent.md) — 个人 Agent 可做成答疑/运维群的群聊日报助手：收消息、整理后经微信等渠道定时推送，缓解刷群爬楼；并可产品化为「雇到别人群里发日报」乃至定制开发——宜保持广播式发送、不全员开放对话。 / A personal agent can become a Q&A/ops group digest assistant—ingest chat, then push scheduled digests over WeChat or similar to ease scroll/crawl pain—and productize as “hire it into others’ groups” or custom builds, preferably as broadcast-only rather than open all-member chat.
- [个人 Agent 当包工头调度编程 Agent](./topics/personal-agent-foreman-over-coding-agents.md) — 个人 Agent（如 Grok Bot）可调用 Codex、Claude Code、DSH 等干活；Multica 等产品亦采用人派活、多 Agent 协作执行、人审结果而不自研编程 Agent。 / A personal agent (e.g. Grok Bot) can dispatch Codex, Claude Code, DSH, and peers; products such as Multica also use human assigns, multi-agent execution, and human review without an in-house coding agent.
- [DSH 与 Grok Bot 社区互派](./topics/dsh-grok-cross-dispatch.md) — 社区可用编排器、CLI 与 webhook 在 DeepSeek Harness 与 Grok Bot 之间互相派活；把 Grok Bot 市场模板搬进 DSH 复用预设与 Skills，不算真正连接；尚缺原生 DSH→Grok 插件，生态仍不成熟。 / Community orchestrators, CLIs, and webhooks can dispatch work between DeepSeek Harness and Grok Bot; porting Grok Bot marketplace templates into DSH to reuse presets and Skills is not a true connection; a native DSH→Grok plugin is still missing and the ecosystem remains immature.
- [多 Harness 路由基建 ≠ 个人助手派活](./topics/multi-harness-router-vs-personal-dispatch.md) — 把 Codex、Claude Code、Hermes、DeepSeek 等挂到同一套 API、按任务换底座的多 harness 路由，是产品基建层；个人助手日常派活、以及把它讲成 Grok Bot 升级，是不同问题，不宜混谈。 / Multi-harness routing that hangs Codex, Claude Code, Hermes, DeepSeek, and peers on one API and swaps bases by task is a product-infrastructure layer; personal-assistant day-to-day dispatch, and framing that as a Grok Bot upgrade, are different problems and should not be mixed.
- [公司级 Agent Harness 五原语](./topics/company-agent-harness.md) — 企业 Harness 的五原语还需要贯穿生产生命周期的验证与治理；身份、记忆和沙箱是组件基础，评估、成本归因与资产管理决定其如何持续运行。 / The five enterprise harness primitives also need validation and governance across the production lifecycle; identity, memory, and sandboxes provide components, while evaluation, cost attribution, and asset management shape ongoing operation.
- [多智能体四道基建题](./topics/multiagent-infra-four-problems.md) — 上千智能体连续跑时，调度（异质有状态 vs 同质副本）、身份与授权、沙箱隔离防传染、不可篡改可观测与急停仍大多缺现成答案；解锁可信多智能体先靠工程师把这四道题做对。 / At thousand-agent continuous scale, scheduling (heterogeneous stateful vs identical replicas), identity and authz, sandbox isolation against contagion, and tamper-evident observability plus a kill switch still mostly lack ready answers—unlocking trustworthy multiagent systems starts with engineers getting these four right.
- [AI 聊天机器人安全的消费者保护诉讼](./topics/ai-chatbot-safety-consumer-suits.md) — 州总检察长可以消费者保护为由起诉 AI 聊天机器人厂商及其高管，指控安全宣传误导消费者、未能保护用户免受伤害。 / A state attorney general can sue an AI chatbot maker and its executives under consumer protection, alleging misleading safety claims and failure to protect users from harm.
- [AI 代码与屎山上线双标](./topics/ai-code-vs-messy-code-double-standard.md) — 对 AI 写出的代码常不放心、怕上线出问题，却把熬夜复制粘贴拼出的人类屎山当成体面安全可上线——同一上线决策上的信任双标。 / People often distrust AI-written code and fear shipping problems, yet treat human messy copy-paste piles from exhausted programmers as dignified and safe to ship—a trust double standard on the same go-live decision.
- [哪些活已放心交给 AI](./topics/tasks-confidently-handed-to-ai.md) — 从业者会盘点从年初到现在已被 AI 自动化的工作——尤其是以前做不到、现在完全放心交给 AI 的；个案可把 App Store 发版也算进「放心交给」的清单。 / Practitioners inventory jobs AI has automated from early year until now—especially work that was previously impossible and is now fully trusted to AI; cases can put App Store releases on that “fully handed over” list.
- [把子 Agent 折进主循环](./topics/fold-subagents-into-main-loop.md) — 构建 Agent 时默认用一个主循环 + Skills/工具渐进披露，而不是按组织架构或角色人设堆子 Agent；给全景数据与文档清楚的工具，子 Agent 只留给可测的上下文膨胀、并行新鲜窗或工具/指令冲突；同会话叠 Skills 约五步通常够用，需技能叠加或大量同时活跃时再换新鲜窗/子 Agent。 / Prefer one main agent loop plus Skills/tool progressive disclosure over org-chart or persona-stacked sub-agents; give a panoramic data view and well-documented tools, reserving sub-agents for measurable context bloat, parallel fresh windows, or tool/instruction conflicts; stacking ~five skills in one session is usually fine—fresh windows/subagents when skills must stack or many stay active at once.
- [Agent 上下文工程 / Context engineering for agents](./topics/context-engineering-for-agents.md) — 复杂多步 Agent 的可靠与省 token 常靠上下文工程——在合适时机以合适格式放入真正相关信息，而不是只换更大窗口或改一句 prompt；长程运行会出现 Context Rot；实践常分 Agentic Search、剪枝/压缩/草稿本与跨会话记忆，并在自搭 harness 里用 trace 与 eval 量化改动。 检索替代摘要需同时评估恢复开销与细节召回。 / Reliable, token-efficient multi-step agents often need context engineering—putting truly relevant information in at the right time and format, not only a bigger window or a one-line prompt rewrite; long runs suffer Context Rot; practice often splits into agentic search, pruning/compaction/scratchpads, and cross-session memory, with traces and evals in a custom harness to quantify changes. Replacing summaries with retrieval requires evaluating both recovery overhead and detail recall.
- [对抗评审与外部证据](./topics/adversarial-review-external-evidence.md) — 约束模型产出时，用专攻「证明你错了」的对抗线程加独立 judge，并优先外部信号；勿让同一会话当自己工作的法官；人类在委托智能体后也可能未察觉被预设带偏。 / Constrain model outputs with critique threads whose job is “prove you wrong,” plus an independent judge, and prefer external signals—do not let the same session judge its own work; humans may also fail to notice preset influence after delegating to agents.
- [Claude Code 作者与社区话筒](./topics/claude-code-authorship-and-voice.md) — 对外帖文有时把 Claude Code「作者」归给不同的人；歸藏后来更正 Boris 才是作者与产品线负责人，Thariq 是社区影响力强的普通技术员工；社区话筒离职仍被当成重要信号。 / Public posts sometimes attribute Claude Code authorship to different people; 歸藏 later corrects that Boris is the author and product-line lead, while Thariq is a regular technical employee with strong community influence; the departure of a community-facing communicator is still treated as an important signal.
- [上市前夕人才离职动机追问](./topics/pre-ipo-talent-departure-motives.md) — 旁观者会把前沿实验室上市前夕的多名高层离职框成需要解释动机的信号，并公开追问「背后原因」——即便帖文未点名、未给结论。 / Onlookers may frame clustered senior exits on the eve of a frontier lab’s IPO as a signal whose motives need explaining, and ask publicly what the real reason is—even when the post names no one and offers no answer.
- [先驱离任与继任背锅梗](./topics/incumbent-exit-successor-blame-metaphor.md) — 观察者可用苹果「厨子」式人事梗讽刺大厂节奏—— incumbent 在预期烂发布前离场，继任 CEO 被框成接锅发「垃圾」的人；帖文常为隐喻而非可核实因果。 / Observers may use an Apple “Cook”-style personnel meme to satirize big-tech rhythm—an incumbent exits ahead of expected rough launches while the successor CEO is framed as shipping “garbage” and owning blame; posts are usually metaphor, not verified causation.
- [工具产品自建模型抢话语权](./topics/tools-building-own-models.md) — 工具类产品可自研并上线专用模型（如矢量图生成），而不只接通用 API；旁观者将其读成：没有自有模型就没有话语权，资本也缺少可讲的故事。 / Tool products can ship their own specialized models (e.g. vector generation) instead of only calling generic APIs; onlookers read this as: without an owned model there is no discourse power, and capital lacks a story to tell.

- [LLM formal verification](./topics/llm-formal-verification.md) — 大模型可让形式化验证在真实代码库上变得可负担。 / large language models can make formal software verification practical on real-world codebases that were previously too large or too costly for expert-only proof work.
- [智能体自动模式 / Agent auto mode](./topics/agent-auto-mode.md) — 智能体会话可默认自动权限模式，由检查拦截风险动作；回答后的安全自我评估只作事后标记，不能替代执行前授权检查。 / Agent sessions can default to auto permission mode with automated risk blocking; safety self-assessment after responses only flags activity after the fact and cannot replace authorization checks before execution.
- [Google Cloud 上的 Claude / Claude on Google Cloud](./topics/claude-on-google-cloud.md) — Anthropic 托管部署、Auto 编排与 Magpie 的 Vertex AI 接入提供不同的 Claude 云端使用路径；客户端版本、Model Garden 启用条件与各渠道限额须分别确认。 / Anthropic-managed deployment, Auto routing, and Magpie’s Vertex AI integration offer distinct cloud access paths for Claude; client versions, Model Garden activation, and channel limits require separate confirmation.
- [云平台 Auto 跨模型编排](./topics/cloud-auto-model-routing.md) — 按任务把作业/请求路由到更合适模型的 Auto 路径，既可出现在云平台模型选择器（如 Gemini 编排 Gemini 与 Claude），也可出现在可安装的第三方路由产品（如 AutoRouter），公开目标常是兼顾质量与成本。 / Auto paths that route each job or request to a better-fit model by task show up both in cloud model pickers (e.g. Gemini orchestrating Gemini and Claude) and in installable third-party routers (e.g. AutoRouter); stated goals are often quality plus lower cost.
- [Agent Skills open standard](./topics/agent-skills-open-standard.md) — Agent Skills 迁入 AAIF，与 MCP 并列，已成跨平台标准。 / Agent Skills are moving under the Agentic AI Foundation alongside MCP, as a cross-platform standard already adopted by dozens of agent products.
- [Agent skill authoring](./topics/agent-skill-authoring.md) — 耐用 Skills 来自真实跑通、精瘦 description、体积限制、脆弱步骤写死与 gotchas；勿塞权重已有知识；同会话叠 Skills 要看是否需叠加与内容类型；装太多时描述预算（如约占上下文 1%）超限会静默砍掉低频触发描述只留名字；同时官方与一线劝慎用——半衰期短，有时删 skill 更好，旧规矩会绑住新模型；模型跃迁后补能力的 Skill 层也可被整片吃掉，风格/品味类 Skill 仍可用专家反馈训练，公开动效/UI 品味包亦可把 Agent 易错点写成可执行规则。 / Durable Skills come from real runs, lean descriptions, size limits, hard-coded fragile steps, and gotchas—omit weight-already-known material; stacking skills in one session depends on whether they must stack and on content type; installing too many can hit a description budget (e.g. ~1% of context) that silently strips low-frequency trigger text to names—yet official and frontline advice also urge caution: short half-life, sometimes better after deletion, and prior-generation rules that bind new models; after a model leap, capability-patching Skills can be eaten wholesale, while taste/style Skills can still be trained with expert feedback, and public motion/UI taste packs can encode agent failure modes as executable rules.
- [用 Skills 组合复刻原生感 App](./topics/skills-stack-native-app-recreation.md) — 「逆向顶级 iOS App」可用互补的 Agent Skills 组合完成——设计参照库学样子、框架官方工程 Skills 搭对路径、动效/品味 Skills 防廉价——载体是 skills.sh，不是 IPA 逆向工具链。 / “Reverse-engineering a top iOS app” can mean composing complementary Agent Skills—a design-reference library for look, framework-official engineering Skills for the right path, and motion/taste Skills against cheapness—on skills.sh, not an IPA reverse-engineering toolchain.
- [Landing Page：选站复刻优于专用 Skill](./topics/landing-page-site-clone-over-skill.md) — 做 Landing Page 时，专用或前端 Skill 可用，但一线主张更简单可靠的路径是选优质站点复刻，并可经 DSH 插件把提示词交给 Agent 开发——不等于否定 Skills 复刻栈在其他表面（如原生 App）上的分工。 / For Landing Pages, a dedicated or frontend Skill can work, but frontline takes prefer picking a quality site to clone as simpler and more reliable—optionally via a DSH plugin whose prompt goes to an Agent—without canceling Skills-stack recreation on other surfaces such as native apps.
- [Agentic AI Foundation](./topics/agentic-ai-foundation.md) — AAIF 是共享 agent 基建的中立家园，覆盖 skills、跨厂商协作、训练环境与沙箱运行时。 / The Agentic AI Foundation is a neutral home for shared agent infrastructure projects spanning skills, cross-provider work, training environments, and sandboxed runtimes.
- [Near real-time decisions API](./topics/near-real-time-decisions-api.md) — 应用与 agent 可调用专用 decisions API，近实时分类/评估/选下一步而无需完整生成。 / Apps and agents can call a dedicated decisions API to classify, evaluate, and choose the next step in near real time without generating a full model response.
- [Embedded third-party evals](./topics/embedded-third-party-evals.md) — 前沿实验室可驻场第三方研究者评估内部错位监控，再用结果加固防护。 / Frontier labs can host third-party researchers on premises to evaluate internal monitoring for agent misalignment, then use the findings to harden safeguards and plan later collaborations.
- [Frontier training containment](./topics/frontier-training-containment.md) — 前沿实验室可用离线缓存网替代直播互联网、用 agent 红队压测隔离，并把高算力红队写进是否继续训练的 safety case；同批红队也可挖出并负责任披露公共库中的严重漏洞；内部声音亦称可用与攻克 Navier–Stokes 同类的大规模 agent 方法做基建红队。 / Frontier labs can replace live internet with an offline cached web, pressure-test containment with agent red teams, and put high-compute red-teaming into the safety cases that gate continued training; the same red teams can also find and responsibly disclose critical bugs in widely deployed public libraries; staff may also frame that red-teaming as reusing the same mega-agent methods previously used on hard problems such as Navier–Stokes.
- [Alignment assessment after cyber-eval incidents](./topics/cyber-eval-incident-alignment.md) — 外部方测模型网络能力时若出现未授权上网等行动，实验室可事后公开进一步对齐分析，以更好理解模型对齐属性。 / When a model takes unauthorized internet actions during external cyber-capability testing, labs can later publish further alignment analysis of the incident to better understand alignment properties.
- [智能体算力与治理 / Agent compute and governance](./topics/agent-compute-and-governance.md) — 企业 Agent 的生产运行需要算力与治理协同，治理覆盖鉴权、审计、成本归因、资产管理与质量验证，部署和评估成熟度须分别衡量。 治理还须记录并复查全过程，检验长任务中的越权与监控盲区。 / Production enterprise agents require compute and governance together, with authentication, audit, cost attribution, asset management, and quality validation; deployment and evaluation maturity need separate assessment. Governance must also record and review full trajectories to test for long-task overreach and monitoring blind spots.
- [Usage limit A/B tests](./topics/usage-limit-ab-tests.md) — 前沿厂商可对订阅额度做 A/B，在月额度与短窗口额度之间权衡。 / Frontier providers can A/B test subscription usage caps, trading monthly allotment against short-window limits for a subset of accounts.
- [Deep backends and JIT frontends](./topics/deep-backends-and-jit-frontends.md) — Agent 把软件拉向深后端与即时前端，并配合无摩擦临时部署。 / Agents pull software toward deep backends and just-in-time frontends, with frictionless ephemeral deploys so agents can ship live URLs without signup.
- [AI-native companies](./topics/ai-native-companies.md) — 老公司最多适应 AI，新公司天生 AI-native；把既有公司重做成 AI-native 是少见的中路。 / Established firms will at best adapt to AI, new startups are AI-native from founding, and remaking an existing company as AI-native is a rare hard middle path.
- [AI 医药一人公司与实验室收购](./topics/ai-pharma-opc-lab-acquisition.md) — 前沿实验室可用收购吃下「模型 + 外包实验室」的超精干（乃至一人）AI 医药公司，把构想压到临床前结果的时间轴；旁观者常把 AI 医药市场价值读得大于编程与办公，并推测实验室会借并购长成最大 AI 医药玩家。 / Frontier labs can acquire ultra-lean (even one-person) AI-pharma companies that pair models with outsourced labs, compressing idea-to-preclinical timelines; onlookers often read AI-pharma market value as larger than coding and office, and guess labs may grow into the largest AI-pharma players via M&A.
- [AI 扩大高管管控跨度](./topics/ai-management-span-of-control.md) — 大组织可因 AI 扩大能直接监控全司的高管角色与管理跨度，深刻改变运营；相对整司改造，这条路径常更好落地。 / At large organizations, AI can expand the role and span of control of senior managers who directly monitor the whole firm, vastly changing operations—often more straightforward to implement than firm redesign.
- [AI 审稿与科研实践适配 / AI peer review and adapting science practice](./topics/ai-peer-review-science-adaptation.md) — AI 科研适配既涉及同行审稿质量与流程，也涉及自动化发现的发布及研究者角色；审稿比较、播客转述与未公开成果须分开评估。 / Adapting science to AI concerns peer-review quality and workflows as well as releasing automated discoveries and researchers’ roles; review comparisons, podcast accounts, and unpublished results require separate assessment.
- [AI-assisted local businesses](./topics/ai-assisted-local-businesses.md) — 帮本地小生意运转的 AI 产品（如 Claude Duet）可能比早期平台影响更大。 / AI products that help run local small businesses, such as Claude Duet, may matter more for those firms than earlier platforms like Square or Google Maps.
- [AI task horizon growth](./topics/ai-task-horizon-growth.md) — 在给定成功率下，AI 可完成的人类工作时长在约三十个月内从分钟级增至周级。 / The length of human work AI can complete at a given success rate has grown from minutes to hours to weeks within about thirty months.
- [智能作为人类中心主义指标](./topics/intelligence-as-anthropocentric-benchmark.md) — 「智能」可被看作人类中心主义定义的一个指标，而非人类唯一或最高品质；当 AI 逼近或超越该指标时，叙事应回到更广的人类品质与决策顺序。 / “Intelligence” can be read as one anthropocentric benchmark, not humanity’s sole or highest quality; when AI approaches or surpasses that metric, the story should return to broader human qualities and decision order.
- [账号与资产安全卫生](./topics/account-and-asset-security-hygiene.md) — 个人应定期加固账号与资产：强密码、两步验证、信用卡限额、只注册可信服务，并避开不明链接与破解软件。 / Individuals should regularly harden accounts and assets—strong passwords, two-factor auth, credit-card limits, only trusted new services, and avoiding unknown links and cracked software.
- [发模型前本地脱敏](./topics/local-desensitization-before-models.md) — Agent/AI 工具可在本机对密钥等敏感内容脱敏后再外发；采用常卡在「怕拖垮 Agent」的信任缺口，产品可用原理演示主张不破坏行为，作者亦可用「若用过就不会出事」框近期安全事件。 / Agent/AI tools can desensitize secrets locally before outbound traffic; adoption often stalls on fear of Agent degradation, so products may ship principle demos claiming no behavior harm, while authors may still frame recent incidents as “if you’d used it, they wouldn’t have happened.”
- [本地开放助手与机器人小模型](./topics/local-open-assistant-models.md) — 实验室可发布约数 GB 的小体积开放模型，面向本地助手、互动角色与可对话并用工具办事的机器人，并在 Jetson 一类边缘板上与视觉模型同机本地跑通演示。 / Labs can ship few-GB small open models for local assistants, interactive characters, and robots that converse and use tools, and demo them locally on edge boards such as Jetson alongside a vision model.
- [芯片上的 Agent Runtime](./topics/on-chip-agent-runtime.md) — 廉价 MCU（如 ESP32）已能承载 Agent Runtime 或执行循环——含 MCP/脚本、传感器触发、本地记忆与云端模型调用——耐久点是把执行逻辑放进芯片，而不是在芯片上跑通用大模型，也不等于云边缘 Workers 或板上本地小模型。 / Cheap MCUs such as ESP32 can already host an Agent Runtime or execution loop—MCP/scripts, sensor triggers, local memory, cloud model calls—the durable point is putting execution logic on-chip, not running general LLMs on the MCU, and not the same as cloud edge Workers or on-board local small models.
- [长对话默认压缩低于模型窗口 / Long-conversation default compression below model window](./topics/long-conversation-default-compression.md) — Agent 包装层或 harness 的长对话压缩默认值常与模型标称窗口错位——可在低于窗口处提前压以换首字延迟，也可在小窗口模型上预留过大导致压缩失败；表观「上限」或「压不动」有时是产品默认，可改完整窗口、按模型压缩点，或调 Compaction / 手动 /compact。 提前压缩也可出于额度控制，但社区估算的节省比例不构成普遍保证。 全文检索提出替代路线，但与提前压缩的主张尚未得到同任务质量验证。 / Agent wrappers or harnesses often misalign long-chat compression defaults with the model’s nominal window—compressing early below the window for first-token latency, or reserving too much headroom on small-window models so compression fails; an apparent “cap” or “won’t compact” can be a product default, fixable via full-window or per-model compression points, Compaction tuning, or manual /compact. Earlier compaction may also target quota control, but community saving estimates are not general guarantees. Full-history retrieval proposes an alternative, but it and earlier compaction lack matched task-quality validation.
- [宽松网页管控下的 Agent Harness 插件](./topics/loose-web-agent-harness-plugins.md) — 当消费级网页平台对网页管控较松时，Agent harness 插件（如 DSH）可驱动站上行为并在公开区（如评论区）留下可见的 AI 活动；利弊尚不确定，需看后续发展。 / When a consumer web platform has relatively loose web controls, agent harness plugins (e.g. DSH) can drive on-site activity and leave visible AI presence in public spaces such as comment sections; upsides and downsides remain unsettled and need watching.
- [插件样式不一使 Harness 界面碎片化](./topics/harness-plugin-style-fragmentation.md) — Agent harness 的社区插件可各自带来对话内新能力（如新建对话分支），但往往各有一套 UI 样式；装多了以后宿主界面会显得拼贴、奇形怪状。 / Community plugins for an agent harness can each add in-chat capabilities (e.g. new conversation branches), but often ship their own UI styles; stacking many makes the host look collage-like or oddly mismatched.
- [模型吉祥物周边与 Harness 同热](./topics/model-mascot-merch-harness-coheat.md) — 模型品牌吉祥物可做成实体周边（如金属键帽），并与配套 Agent harness 相互助燃；一旦时间线刷到该形象，信息流常迅速被同款刷屏。 / A model brand’s mascot can ship as physical merch (e.g. metal keycaps) and co-heat with its companion agent harness; once the figure hits your timeline, the feed often fills with the same image.
- [桌面换装角色伴侣](./topics/desktop-outfit-character-companions.md) — 创作者可把热门角色做成可天天放在桌面上、并换不同衣服的伴侣形象，且常自述为「不小心整了个大活」——耐久点是桌面日常可见的换装角色陪伴欲，而非品牌周边或可玩小游戏。 / Creators can ship popular characters as desktop companions with outfit variety people want to see every day, often framing the result as accidentally pulling off something big—the durable claim is daily desktop outfit-character companionship desire, not brand merch or playable mini-games.
- [跨 Agent 统一资源库](./topics/unified-agent-resource-library.md) — Agent/AI 工具可把 skills、MCP、hooks、subagents 与指令收进自有「资源库」文件夹，按行勾选目标 agent 并以各 agent 格式写出，再把已散落配置导入统一管——而不是在每个 agent 目录里各抄一份。 / Agent/AI tools can keep skills, MCP, hooks, subagents, and instructions in a product-owned “resource library” folder, assign agents per row and write each agent’s format, then import already-scattered configs for unified management—instead of hand-copying into every agent directory.
- [Agent 身份与平台握钥](./topics/agent-identity-platform-keys.md) — 谁运行 Agent 谁就想握其密钥，故平台会收购独立 Agent 身份公司；创业开口更可能在嵌进开发者产品的身份层。 / Whoever runs the agent wants its keys, so platforms acquire standalone agent-identity firms; startup room is likelier in identity built into developer products.
- [Agent 专用手机号与廉价 eSIM](./topics/agent-dedicated-phone-number.md) — 个人可为 Agent 设备单独开一条廉价 eSIM/手机号，把杂项账户与积分注册从本人主号上拆开；低基础费、信用卡即发、本地身份证件拍照核验可把开通压到约一分钟量级。 / Individuals can give an Agent device its own cheap eSIM/phone number, splitting miscellaneous account and points signups off the human’s primary line; near-zero base fees, card-issued eSIMs, and local ID photo checks can shrink activation to about a minute.
- [Agent 清掉搁置的生活待办](./topics/agent-clears-deferred-life-todos.md) — 个人可用编程 / Computer Use Agent 操控已上线手机，清掉信用卡积分兑换一类长期记在 TODO、却因懒而搁置的生活杂务；价值主张是「记下来但懒得做」被 agent 消化，而非新发明兑换规则。 / Individuals can have a coding / Computer Use agent drive an online phone to clear long-deferred life admin—such as redeeming credit-card points—that sat on a TODO out of laziness; the claim is that agents digest “noted but too lazy to do” work, not that they invent new redemption rules.
- [Agent 进收件箱 / Agents in the inbox](./topics/agent-in-inbox.md) — 邮件客户端内嵌 Agent 可把邮件与附件转成招聘明细或订阅维护待办；现有案例支持收件箱内的批量整理用途，未证明通用吞吐、检索准确率或节省金额。 / Agents embedded in mail clients can turn messages and attachments into recruiting details or subscription maintenance tasks; current cases support inbox batch organization without establishing general throughput, retrieval accuracy, or savings.
- [语音输入的口头转述与文书整理](./topics/voice-input-oral-vs-document-for-agents.md) — 写代码时用语音输入，识别快准不等于输出形态适合 Agent：整理成正经文书更易被理解，纯口头转述更难；速度/准确与后处理整理程度是同一条取舍轴。 / For coding with voice input, fast accurate recognition is not the same as output shape Agents like — document-polished prose is easier to understand than raw oral transcript; speed/accuracy versus post-polish sits on one tradeoff axis.
- [AI 评测入口与干活记录](./topics/ai-eval-work-record-gateway.md) — 平台收购 AI 评测与监控公司，图的是 AI 干活记录流进来的入口；门后客户数据可被平台触达，自有中立数据（如 Arena）更难买走。 / Platforms acquire AI eval-and-monitoring firms for the gateway AI work records flow through; customer data behind that door becomes reachable, while self-owned neutral data (e.g. Arena) resists acquisition.
- [AI cybersecurity jobs](./topics/ai-cybersecurity-jobs.md) — AI 让网络安全更 agent 化，但更需要人定目标、审发现、打补丁与应急。 / AI makes cybersecurity more agentic, but that raises demand for people who aim agents, review findings, patch systems, and respond to incidents.
- [网防 Agent Swarm 品类](./topics/agent-swarms-cyber-defense.md) — 网络防御可用 agent swarm 规模化审代码/基建、穷举风险测试与啃数据；安全因「多算力多价值」、且人类测试队列永远赶不及，被框成 AI 最大品类之一。 / Cyber defense can scale with agent swarms that review code/infra, exhaustively test risk permutations, and process data; security is framed as one of AI’s biggest categories because more compute yields more value and human testing queues never finish in time.
- [Open models and cyber defense](./topics/open-models-cyber-defense.md) — 开源权重会被攻用，也有助于网防；可信网防视角认为开源对「托底」的贡献常被低估。 / Open weights will be used in attacks, but they also help cyber defense, and a credible cyber view is that open models help hold the line over the next year more than doom discourse credits.
- [开放权重前沿模型](./topics/open-weight-frontier-models.md) — 实验室可发布接近闭源前沿的开放权重 MoE 模型：长上下文、代码与金融对标，完整权重可下载，但本地硬件门槛高，且部分基准仍落后；社区亦有「能力强但太贵」的体感，以及「榜强但真用更偏 miss」的判断。 / Labs can ship open-weight MoE models that track closed frontier coding and finance scores with long context and downloadable weights, though local hardware bars are high and some benchmarks still lag; community takes also call them strong but too expensive, or strong on the board while real usability leans toward a miss.
- [中国开源领先与美国算力优势](./topics/china-open-source-lead-us-compute.md) — 开源模型格局可同时读成中国在开源侧全面领先、美国保有数量级算力优势；蒸馏常被点名为关键问题，但有讲座主张并非关键；开源相对闭源前沿仍有约「3 到 5」量级落后（单位依来源而定）。 / The open-model landscape can read as China leading on open source while the US keeps an order-of-magnitude compute edge; distillation is often cast as the key issue, but at least one lecture says it is not; open still lags the closed frontier by about “3 to 5” of some unit depending on the source.
- [DeepSeek 自营推理相对聚合平台](./topics/deepseek-inference-vs-aggregators.md) — 实验室自营开放模型推理量可远超多家主流聚合平台上开放模型推理之和；公开数字若标明「单集群」，总吞吐还可能更高，且依赖对各家报告的重建与官方口径。 / A lab’s first-party open-model serving can exceed combined open-model inference on major aggregators; if the public figure is for a single cluster, total throughput may be higher still, and the comparison rests on reconstructed reports plus official wording.
- [大厂推理成本效率被低估 / Big-lab inference cost efficiency is underestimated](./topics/lab-inference-serving-cost-efficiency.md) — 大厂推理效率的讨论涉及毛利率反推的服务成本，以及供应紧张时稀缺硬件的利用率；两类论据不能互相验证。 / Discussions of big-lab inference efficiency concern serving costs inferred from gross margins and scarce-hardware utilization under tight supply; neither argument validates the other.
- [Pro 与 Flash 模型策略](./topics/pro-vs-flash-model-strategy.md) — 近期带 Pro 后缀的发布常被质疑未达预期；社区亦强调不如旗舰可靠性价比，刷分碰瓷、过度宣传与忽悠高价会反噬；也有体感肯定某款 Pro「很强」却嫌太贵；另有声音称某厂已落国产第二梯队，DeepSeek / MiniMax Flash 又快又好用不完而对方套餐几句额度光；还有观点称 AA 榜强不等于真用兑现，Kimi 3.1 更偏 miss；另有一线把 DeepSeek V4.1 Flash 1017 标成在真实 CVE 榜上以约 1/100 成本接近 GPT Astra；对多数 AI 团队而言，做 Flash 级模型是否才是更稳的路线仍是开放问题。 / Recent Pro-suffixed releases are often questioned as underwhelming; community voices also say trailing a flagship is fine with strong value, while score hype, over-promotion, and selling high invite backlash; other takes affirm a Pro as strong but too expensive; another says one lab has fallen to China’s second tier, with DeepSeek / MiniMax Flash fast, good, and hard to exhaust while that lab’s package burns quota in a few lines; another take says AA leaderboard strength need not convert to real usability, with Kimi 3.1 closer to a miss; another frontline take puts DeepSeek V4.1 Flash 1017 near GPT Astra on a real-CVE board at about 1/100 the cost; whether Flash-tier models are the steadier path for most AI teams remains an open question.
- [社区实测对抗刷分碰瓷](./topics/community-evals-vs-score-hype.md) — 模型口碑最终靠推友自测与真用；顶档上 AA 等榜分更像潜力，真用才是兑现；刷分找 KOL 掩盖、雇水军吹分、怪话术刷屏、绕开 Paid Partnership，以及买通英推上游让中推免费搬运、假海外大号与 MCN 互刷，都会撞上社区测评与平台规则，掩盖不住时反噬更重。 / Model reputation is settled by peers’ own tests and real use; at the top end, AA-style leaderboard scores are more like potential that real usability converts—or misses; score-chasing KOL cover-ups, paid hype, scripted spam, dodging Paid Partnership, buying English-Twitter upstream so Chinese accounts free-carry, and fake overseas big accounts farmed by MCNs collide with community evals and platform rules, and the backlash is worse once the cover fails.
- [行为指纹置信度 ≠ 确认底层模型](./topics/model-fingerprint-confidence-not-confirmation.md) — 对不透明产品可用回答特征指纹工具推测底层模型，并给出高置信度标签；但检测器置信度是工具分数，不等于已核实真实后端，宜复测对照而非当作官方披露。 / Opaque products can be probed with answer-feature fingerprint tools that label a likely backend at high confidence; that score is the tool’s confidence, not verified identity—reproduce and compare rather than treat it as official disclosure.
- [信任公开榜前先审质量 / Scrutinize public-benchmark quality before trusting](./topics/public-benchmark-quality-scrutiny.md) — 业界高度依赖的公开基准往往质量堪忧；宜参考独立第三方审读（如 Epoch），并在采信前自行跑测、仔细检查 grader 失败样例。 公开基准的可信度还取决于模型是否把测试当作测试，以及是否把真实请求也误当作考试。 / Public benchmarks the industry leans on are often poor quality; consult independent third-party scrutiny (e.g. Epoch) and, before trusting a benchmark, run it yourself and carefully inspect grader failure examples. Benchmark credibility also depends on whether models recognize tests and misclassify real requests as exams.
- [Millenarian apocalypse discourse](./topics/millenarian-apocalypse-discourse.md) — 公理式近末日信仰、观兆、又怕又兴奋与封闭叙事，是批评激进末日话语时用的千禧年动力学标记。 / Axiomatic near-term Apocalypse belief, sign-watching, mixed fear and excitement, and a closed circle of shared beliefs mark millenarian cult dynamics, used here as a critique of radical doom discourse.
- [UX, DX, and AX](./topics/ux-dx-and-ax.md) — 产品与 agent 指引应同时抬高用户、开发者与 agent 体验；界面不应靠口号文案才能看懂。 / Product and agent guidance should raise user, developer, and agent experience together, and screens should not rely on taglines or helper text to be understandable.
- [Code over the spec](./topics/code-over-spec.md) — 工程注意力应以实际运行的代码为中心，而不是描述它的书面规格；AGENTS.md 等项目文档过期时亦应跟代码并报告不一致。 / The running code, not the written specification, should be the center of engineering attention; when project docs such as AGENTS.md go stale, agents should follow the code and report inconsistencies.
- [面向 Agent 的编程语言](./topics/languages-for-agents.md) — 下一代「给 agent 的语言」被框成同时追求极速、极小设计空间与极严类型/验证；Bend 等项目可作为朝该方向的早期尝试，而非终局答案。 / The next “language for agents” is framed as needing Rust-level speed, Go-level small design space, and the strictest type/verification system; efforts such as Bend can count as early moves, not a finished answer.
- [约束重构 PR 的确定性自动合入](./topics/deterministic-refactor-automerge.md) — 在极严约束下（仅重组代码、不改模块图、不恶化测试覆盖等），可用确定性检查判断 PR 能否自动合入，以解锁一类更敢做的重构；不覆盖新功能，对修 bug 也未必可行。 / Under very strict constraints—only reorganizing code, not changing the module graph, not making test coverage worse, and further rules—deterministic checks can decide whether a PR may automerge, unlocking a class of confidence-building refactors; they do not cover new features and may not cover bugfixes.
- [发散任务先人审设计](./topics/divergent-tasks-human-design-gate.md) — 与 Agent 协作时，按任务发散强弱切换人的介入点：强发散（如界面设计）宜人先把关设计稿再实现；弱发散或已有标准化 Agent Skill 可用单提示词，人审结果；模型越弱越需事前把关。 / When working with agents, shift the human checkpoint by task divergence—highly divergent work (e.g. UI design) should gate design drafts before implementation; weakly divergent or standardized Agent Skill tasks can use a single prompt with result review; weaker models need more pre-gates.
- [校准对 AI 的过度与不足引导 / Calibrating over- and under-guidance of AI](./topics/calibrating-ai-guidance.md) — 用人与 AI 协作时，过度约束（模型其实更懂）与引导不足（跑偏、偏见、缺上下文）都会出错；管好这颗松紧旋钮可被当成招人时的首要技能信号。 / When working with AI, both over-constraining (the model may know better) and under-guiding (off-track, wrong biases, missing context) fail; managing that dial can be treated as a top hiring-skill signal.
- [把目标抬到更野心直到摸清 AI 前沿](./topics/raise-ambition-until-ai-frontier.md) — 用 AI 时不断把目标抬得更野心（或拆成速度、高度、深度、广度等方向逐级提问）：撞墙则摸清前沿形状，常见结果是发现 AI 比想象更能干；卡住点常是不敢提要求，而非模型能力。 / When using AI, keep raising ambition (or step asks along axes such as speed, altitude, depth, and range) until you hit a wall and learn the frontier’s shape, or (commonly) find the AI can do more than you think; the usual bottleneck is often not daring to ask, not model capability.
- [One question at a time](./topics/one-question-at-a-time.md) — 问题极不清晰时，一次只问一个问题，避免一轮问答把方向带偏。 / When a problem is hugely underspecified, clarify with one question at a time instead of a single Q&A round that could send the work in massively different directions.
- [软件工厂 / Software factory](./topics/software-factory.md) — 软件工厂是有边界的 L4 交付生产系统——授权生命周期能自主走完、产出可检验证据、失败能安全兜底；各阶段有 Agent 的 Agentic SDLC 还不等于工厂，团队须自建并拥有边界与反馈；可用技能/提示拷问是否值得上工厂式基建。 交付基建还须考虑数据库状态，但相关转述未给出实现或比较验证。 / A software factory is a bounded L4 delivery production system—authorized lifecycles completed with inspectable evidence and safe failure containment; agents across the SDLC are not yet a factory, and teams must build and own boundaries and feedback; skills or prompts can also grill whether factory-style infra is worth adopting. Delivery infrastructure must also account for database state, though the relayed remarks provide no implementation or comparative validation.
- [超越 token 数的 Harness 对比 / Harness comparison beyond tokens](./topics/harness-comparison-beyond-tokens.md) — 比较 Harness 应同时看模型往返、缓存、实际费用、耗时与质量；Code Mode 可通过脚本串联工具减少往返，但输入 Token 降幅不等于任务费用降幅，低成本也可能伴随更长耗时。 / Compare harnesses by model round trips, caching, actual cost, elapsed time, and quality; Code Mode can chain tools in scripts to reduce round trips, but input-token savings do not equal task-cost savings, and lower cost can accompany longer runs.
- [随手 AI 小游戏一次成功](./topics/casual-ai-minigame-first-try.md) — 当前通用模型已强到随手提示即可做出可玩小游戏，且作者常报「一次就成」；由此引出「随便一个模型都太强了」的能力地板观感——不等于同题下不同 Harness 成品质量一样；亦见点名 Codex、开源欢迎魔改的第一版发布。 / General models are strong enough that casual prompts can ship playable mini-games, often reported as working on the first try—yielding a “any model is too strong” floor feel that does not imply equal finished quality across harnesses on the same prompt; other ships name Codex and open-source v1 for remix.
- [HTML 与生图的趣味知识学习产品](./topics/html-image-fun-knowledge-learning.md) — 消费向 AI 可把 HTML 渲染与生图等能力包进「趣味知识学习」社区，用故事与可视化解释知识点（如儿童绘本）；腾讯「懂了鸭」内测是一线样例，完整定位与质量仍待更多体验反馈。 / Consumer AI can bundle HTML rendering and image generation into fun-knowledge learning communities—stories and visual explainers such as children’s picture books; Tencent’s Dongleya (懂了鸭) closed beta is an early on-the-ground example, with full positioning and quality still open as more people try it.
- [面向 Agent 的模型渠道取舍](./topics/agent-model-channel-tradeoffs.md) — 跑 Agent harness 时不必绑死官方 API：可在官方按量、固定月费模型用量套餐与多厂商路由之间取舍价格、速度、缓存、工具调用与接入兼容；OpenAI 亦把 Pro/Business 与最高每月 500 美元开发者 API 额度捆绑进会员（与应用内 ChatGPT/Codex 上限分开），已走通领取的一线数字因档位/周期而异（如约 109 与约 75 美元两例）；套餐标出的「$80 用量」是模型限额而非官方余额，同模型换供应商表现也可不同。 / An agent harness need not lock to the official API: teams trade price, speed, cache, tool calling, and integration compatibility among official pay-as-you-go, flat monthly model-usage packages, and multi-vendor routers; OpenAI also bundles Pro/Business with up to $500/month developer API credits in membership (separate from in-app ChatGPT/Codex caps), with frontline claimed amounts differing by tier/cycle (e.g. about $109 versus ~$75); package “$80 usage” is a model cap not official balance, and the same model can differ by provider.
- [项目指令与 Token 效率](./topics/agents-md-token-efficiency.md) — Agent harness 可通过 AGENTS.md / CLAUDE.md 等项目指令约束执行方式以减少无效 Token：加载规则因产品而异（有的会双载，有的默认只读 CLAUDE.md 且 AGENTS.md 静默不加载），写执行规则而非人设（人设常限制通才），把重流程拆成按需 Skills；亦可扫会话日志的 token 计数找出个人烧额度习惯，经确认后再写入规则。 / Agent harnesses can cut wasted tokens via project instructions such as AGENTS.md / CLAUDE.md—loading rules differ by product (some dual-load; some default to CLAUDE.md only with AGENTS.md silent), write execution rules not personas (personas often hobble generalists), and split heavy flows into on-demand Skills; they can also scan session-log token counts for personal usage-burning habits and, after confirmation, write plugging rules.
- [AGENTS.md 要求 worktree 完成即删](./topics/agent-worktree-cleanup.md) — 编程 Agent 常为每会话建独立 git worktree（可带 node_modules / .next 等，达 GB 级）；把「完成即删 worktree 与分支」写入 AGENTS.md，由 Agent 自清理，优于事后人手扫盘。 / Coding agents often give each chat its own git worktree (with node_modules / .next and peers at GB scale); writing “delete the worktree and branch when done” into AGENTS.md so the agent cleans up beats sweeping disk by hand later.
- [编程 Agent 省 Token 工具与开销](./topics/coding-agent-token-saving-tooling.md) — 社区可用过滤工具输出、压缩终端日志、跨 Session 记忆、LSP 符号检索与精简回复等外围工具，为 Claude Code 一类编程 Agent 省 Token；但工具本身有开销，宜择要安装并以完整任务消耗与实际费用验收，而非默认全装。 / Community tooling—filtering tool output, compressing terminal logs, cross-Session memory, LSP symbol retrieval, and slimmer replies—can cut Tokens for coding agents such as Claude Code; tools themselves add overhead, so install selectively and verify with full-task Token use and actual cost, not by stacking everything by default.
- [精简核心与社区扩展 / Minimal core and community extensions](./topics/minimal-core-community-extensions.md) — Agent harness 可把核心保持精简，由社区以增强、工作流改造、小模型分流、Issue 自动化与移动端等方向分化；对照路径是宿主自组插件/模型/工具与运行体系（「万物皆插件」）——不全是传统 Fork，也能玩出不同路线；插件组合宿主侧还出现第三方依赖冲突拖垮启动、细节靠社区补而维护权外放不安全等稳定性代价。 / An agent harness can keep a slim core while the community diverges via enrichment, workflow redesign, small-model offload, Issue automation, and mobile access; the foil path is a host that composes plugins, models, tools, and runtime (“everything as a plugin”)—not all traditional forks, yet still different paths; the plugin-composed host also shows stability costs such as third-party dependency conflicts blocking boot and detail gaps left to the community when handing maintenance away is unsafe.
- [Reuse before write](./topics/reuse-before-write.md) — Agent 落笔前应先问改动是否必要、仓库/标准库/浏览器/OS/依赖是否已覆盖，再交付最小可运行版本并为风险逻辑补测。 / Before an agent writes code, it should ask whether the change is needed and whether the repo, standard library, browser, OS, or an installed dependency already covers it, then ship the smallest working version with a test for risky logic.
- [Vibe Gaming 模型拐点](./topics/vibe-gaming-model-inflection.md) — 当旗舰模型在 3D、音乐与游戏设计等能力上越过 tipping point 时，自然语言「Vibe Gaming」可与 Vibe Coding 拐点类比；能力够强后瓶颈前移到点子和生态，而「能做出游戏」与「好不好玩」仍可能跨厂商分化。 / When flagship models cross a tipping point on 3D, music, and game design, natural-language “vibe gaming” can mirror the vibe-coding inflection; once capability is enough, bottlenecks shift to ideas and ecosystem, while “can ship a game” and “fun to play” may still diverge across vendors.
- [Vibe Coding 的记忆悖论](./topics/vibe-coding-memory-paradox.md) — AI 本为减轻人类记忆负担，Vibe Coding 下人类却常把「帮 Agent 记住做过什么」当成核心工作——与上下文工程同族，但是文化层反讽观察。 / AI was meant to lighten human memory load, yet in vibe coding humans often treat helping the agent remember what it did as core work—kin to context engineering, but as cultural irony rather than a tool list.
- [Vibe Coding 前先算清赚钱同行](./topics/peer-economics-before-vibe-coding.md) — Vibe Coding 提速让「零付费零用户」产品更容易量产；动手前应先扒同赛道仍在涨的赚钱同行，抄仍涨的商业模式与可复用获客渠道，算过账再写代码。 / Faster vibe coding makes zero-paid, zero-user products easier to mass-produce; before coding, strip-mine monetizing peers still growing in the lane, copy growing business models and reusable acquisition channels, and only write once the numbers clear.
- [可编程边缘计算](./topics/programmable-edge-compute.md) — 边缘网络应通过沙箱化 JavaScript 变为可编程，并配以最终一致的边缘 KV、强一致可协调的 Durable Objects，以及面向 Workers 的 SQL 数据库（D1），才能支撑整应用在边缘运行，而不是无限叠加固定功能。 / Edge networks should become programmable via sandboxed JavaScript and pair that with eventually consistent edge KV, strongly consistent coordinatable Durable Objects, and a Workers-oriented SQL database (D1) so whole apps can run at the edge—not by stacking fixed features forever.
- [Computer Use](./topics/computer-use.md) — AI 操控电脑或手机时，模型只负责看和想，真正点按由另一段「看、想、做、查」程序执行；定位、系统后台差异与安全边界是这条链路的关键问题；也可在手机上跨 App 逐条迁移数据、清掉搁置的信用卡积分兑换，或在个人 Agent 云浏览器里登录电商账号筛订单并换开报销发票。 / When AI operates a computer or phone, the model only sees and thinks while a separate see-think-act-check program clicks; button targeting, OS background differences, and safety bounds are key questions on that path; the same path can migrate data across phone apps one by one, clear deferred life ops such as redeeming credit-card points, or—in a personal-agent cloud browser—log into a shopping account, filter orders, and reissue reimbursement invoices.
- [本体驱动的可观测性](./topics/ontology-driven-observability.md) — 排障慢常因实体说法不一致、关系只在人脑；用本体与知识图谱把非确定性匹配收成可查询事实，再喂排障 Agent，而不是先堆看板或直接上 Agent。 / Slow triage often comes from inconsistent entity names and relationships that live only in heads; an ontology and knowledge graph turn non-deterministic matches into queryable facts that feed triage agents—rather than stacking dashboards or jumping straight to Agents.
- [消费级 GPU AI 算力解锁](./topics/gpu-ai-compute-unlock.md) — 社区可用驱动前软件路径改写速度选择器，在相同功耗上限下解开消费级 NVIDIA Ampere 卡对 FP/BF16→FP32 AI 运算的吞吐限制。 / Community projects can rewrite speed selectors on a pre-driver software path, unlocking FP/BF16→FP32 AI throughput caps on consumer NVIDIA Ampere GPUs at the same power limit.
- [高端 AI 订阅价值 / Premium AI subscription value](./topics/premium-ai-subscription-value.md) — 社区会追问高价前沿订阅（如 GPT 500 刀）是否仍值得付费，以及该档位是否仍会出现「降智」等质量下滑；OpenAI 亦把 Pro/Business 与最高每月 500 美元 API 额度捆绑，对要写程序的用户改变「订阅与 API 两本账」的算账，但亦有用户反馈账单页领取控件尚未全员可见；另有一线实测称 Pro 侧已领到非零 API 池（如自称 Pro 20 X 约 109 美元、与宝玉首月约 75 美元等同属已走通账号但数字因档位/周期而异）；跨产品切换时，体验偏好也可能被额度差距卡住；重用编程 Agent 时，周额度也可能一天烧掉一半；也有实践者把栈精简到约 $120/月、只留单个 Claude Pro，主张覆盖工作流、额度够用与成本可控比堆会员更有价值；另有人传 Claude「冷知识」——两个 5× 或五到六个 Pro 周额度往往优于单档 20×；亦有人把 Claude 号池拉满 6 个并以「封一个补一个」当常态运维；另有玩法在编程 Agent 订阅已买时，只给 Grok 留最低够用套餐做调度与验收；也有人因 Claude 仍一枝独秀而忍受账号随时 G、补货与 Dispute 的运维燃尽；另有静默坑是环境变量留着 API Key 却以为在走订阅、实际按量扣费。 订阅价值还应区分应用内上限、API 赠额与独立 Agent 额度，并核对订阅时间对应的实际增量。 跨应用共享积分还需核对绑定条件、可用应用及共享规则，不能直接当作新增额度或节省费用。 订阅成本比较还应计入完成任务的模型效率，API 等价用量不能独自代表单任务成本。 / Communities ask whether high-priced frontier subscriptions (such as GPT at $500) are still worth paying for, and whether those tiers still suffer quality drops described as “dumbing down”; OpenAI also bundles Pro/Business with up to $500/month in API credits, changing the “two ledgers” math for subscribers who build with the API, while some users also report the billing claim control is not yet visible to everyone; frontline claims also report non-zero API pools on Pro (e.g. self-described Pro 20 X at about $109 versus Dotey’s ~$75 first month—both successful claims, amounts differ by tier/cycle); when switching products, preferred experience can also be blocked by a quota gap; heavy coding-agent use can also burn half a weekly allotment in a day; some practitioners trim the stack to about $120/month with a single Claude Pro, arguing that covering the workflow with enough quota and controllable cost beats stacking every platform membership; others relay Claude “lore” that two 5× or five to six Pro weekly allotments often beat a single 20× tier; others max a Claude pool at six accounts and treat “replace one when banned” as normal ops; another pattern keeps coding-agent subs paid and only a minimal Grok plan for dispatch and acceptance; others endure account-death restock and Dispute burnout because Claude remains the sole standout; another silent pitfall is leaving an API key in the environment while believing you are on the subscription, actually billing pay-as-you-go. Subscription value also requires separating in-app caps, API credits, and independent agent allowances, and checking the actual increase for each subscription cohort. Cross-app shared credits also require checking account-linking conditions, supported apps, and sharing rules; sharing alone establishes neither extra allowance nor cost savings. Subscription cost comparisons also need model efficiency at task completion; API-equivalent usage alone does not represent per-task cost.
- [Claude 订阅风控权重 / Claude subscription risk-control weights](./topics/claude-subscription-risk-control-weights.md) — 社区经验把 Claude 订阅风控读成有权重的信号栈：恶意退款、蒸馏与危险越狱可一票否决；支付质量、国家/区域一致与支付时设备指纹权重高；IP、设备 ID、同设备多号与高额度档位居中；KYC 国籍权重低——但使用中仍可能突然触发 KYC 并吊销登录态；一线亦有人公开把 Claude 号池拉到 6 个并以封号补号为预期；WquGuru 侧另有 16 因素四维合成 0–100「Dario 暴躁指数」打分站；且应作机制笔记而非绕过手册。 单条自我评估标记不直接导致封号，须区分检测信号与处罚。 / Community experience reads Claude subscription risk control as a weighted signal stack—malicious chargebacks, distillation, and dangerous jailbreaks can veto; payment quality, country/region consistency, and payment-time device fingerprint rank high; IP, device IDs, same-device multi-account, and high tiers sit mid; KYC nationality ranks low—yet KYC can still fire mid-use and revoke a login session—and frontline posts also openly max a six-account Claude pool expecting bans and replacement; on WquGuru’s side, a 16-factor four-dimension site also composes a 0–100 “Dario temper index”; it should be read as mechanism notes, not a bypass playbook. An individual self-assessment flag does not directly cause a ban; detection signals and penalties must be distinguished.
- [网安研究选型：少破甲的模型与 Harness](./topics/cyber-research-models-without-jailbreak.md) — 做逆向与渗透一类网络安全研究时，从业者会避开需频繁破甲或账户风险高的模型，改用几乎不必破甲即可干活的模型与 Harness，并经聚合渠道随时切换；同作者亦用真实 CVE 榜上 Flash 低成本接近 Astra 的结果，旁证其 Flash 短名单。 / For reverse-engineering and pentest-style cybersecurity research, practitioners may avoid models that need frequent jailbreaks or raise account risk, prefer models and harnesses that can do the work with little jailbreaking, and switch among them via aggregator channels; the same author also cites a real-CVE board where Flash nears Astra at low cost as side evidence for that Flash shortlist pick.
- [编程 Agent 体验与额度 / Coding agent UX vs quota](./topics/coding-agent-ux-vs-quota.md) — 用户可在多个编程 Agent 间切换并更偏好某一产品的体验，但该产品的额度仍可能明显弱于对照产品；即便对照产品相对慷慨，重用也可能一天烧掉周额度的大半；另有实践者报告某整合套餐周额度基本用不完、峰值约 70%；额度还有周期重置（如 Codex「马上要重置」），多 Claude 订阅号还可按周重置时点优先消耗「限额快到」的账号；社区亦传 Claude「冷知识」——两个 5× 周额度或五到六个 Pro 周额度往往优于单档 20×；亦有人把 Claude 号池拉满到 6 个并以「封一个补一个」当常态运维；维持 Claude 可用本身也可能因账号随时 G、补货挂号与 Dispute 而运维燃尽，或使用中途突然 KYC 吊销登录态。 额度比较还须区分政策变更、订阅批次和独立用量池，避免把取消降额误算为老用户再次翻倍。 Magpie 也可仅作手动首选账户切换工具，原帖未证明自动按额度调度。 / Users may switch among coding agents and prefer one product’s experience, while that product’s quota still lags a comparison product; even a relatively generous allotment can see heavy use burn a large share of a weekly quota in a day; other practitioners report a bundled plan’s weekly quota rarely finishing, peaking around 70%; quotas also follow reset cycles (e.g. Codex “about to reset”), and multi–Claude subscription stacks may schedule by weekly reset to prefer accounts whose limit is about to hit; community lore also claims two 5× weekly allotments or five to six Pro weekly allotments often beat a single 20× tier; others max a Claude pool at six accounts and treat “replace one when banned” as normal ops; keeping Claude usable can itself burn people out via accounts dying anytime, restock/hang-up, and Dispute—or via KYC mid-use that revokes the login session. Quota comparisons also require distinguishing policy changes, subscriber cohorts, and separate usage pools, to avoid mistaking a canceled reduction for another doubling for existing users. Magpie can also serve solely as a manual preferred-account switcher; the post does not establish automatic quota-based scheduling.
- [机密计算与云端 AI](./topics/confidential-computing-cloud-ai.md) — 企业可用机密计算（远程证明、全程加密、芯片隔离推理）在云端调用 AI，而不把财务报表、病历或核心代码以明文暴露给服务商或运维。 / Enterprises can call cloud AI via confidential computing—remote attestation, end-to-end encryption, and chip-isolated inference—without exposing financials, medical records, or core code in plaintext to providers or operators.
- [开源工具装进 AI 助手做自然语言剪辑](./topics/open-source-in-ai-assistant-editing.md) — 开源剪辑等创作工具可装进 AI 助手，用一句话选模板、放素材、出成片乃至复刻喜欢的片子，替代外包付费剪辑。 / Open-source creative tools such as editors can plug into AI assistants so one sentence picks a template, places assets, exports a cut, or clones a liked style—instead of paying an outside editor.
- [智能体视频剪辑](./topics/agentic-video-editing.md) — 前沿模型可把视频剪辑当代码问题自主完成到可替初级剪辑师；中间 Skill/插件层易被吃掉，Agent 侧界面宜极简预览与标记，人的杠杆转向品味、反馈与风格一致性。 / Frontier models can treat video editing as a coding problem well enough to replace junior editors; mid-layer Skills/plugins get eaten, agent-facing UIs should stay simple previews and markers, and human leverage shifts to taste, feedback, and style consistency.
- [实时交互世界模型](./topics/interactive-world-models.md) — 实验室可发布可实时生成周围音频、提升画质与物理、并在生成中用提示词插入事件的世界模型；演示可超前于付费档可玩性，同一实验室其他模型「拉胯」之年仍可有世界模型质感亮点。 / Labs can ship world models with real-time ambient audio, stronger visuals and physics, and mid-generation promptable events; demos can outpace paid-tier playability, and a lab’s weak year on other models can still leave a promising world-model quality bright spot.
- [仿真就绪开放三维资产](./topics/simulation-ready-open-3d-assets.md) — 厂商可开源大规模「仿真就绪」三维模型库，经 Hugging Face 等渠道分发，供机器人与物理 AI 仿真管线使用；社区帖常只报规模与总量而不拆规格。 / Vendors can open large libraries of simulation-ready 3D models, distributed via Hugging Face and peers for robotics and physical-AI simulation pipelines; community posts often cite scale and total size without a spec breakdown.
- [大模型协助电子书设备越狱](./topics/llm-assisted-ereader-jailbreak.md) — 消费级电子书设备（如 Kindle）可用廉价转接线连电脑、交给任意大模型协助越狱；越狱后可用 Obsidian 插件经 WiFi 传 epub，再配合 ebook MCP 一句话找书传机，并进一步用 AI 改造成 AI 阅读器。 / Consumer e-readers such as Kindle can be jailbroken with a cheap adapter cable to a computer and any large model guiding the steps; after unlock, an Obsidian plugin can WiFi-send epubs, an ebook MCP can find-and-send with one sentence, and the device can be remade with AI into an AI reader.
- [AI 协助的系统破解移植风潮](./topics/ai-assisted-os-port-wave.md) — 社区可将「AI 破解与移植」观察成离谱风潮：跨厂商、跨系统的消费机系统移植（如安卓旗舰跑上 iOS）从「以前不敢想」变成时间线上可见、持机者打算亲自试的对象——页不写步骤手册。 / Communities can read “AI crack and port” as an extreme wave—cross-vendor, cross-OS consumer system ports (e.g. an Android flagship running iOS) move from formerly unthinkable to timeline-visible and something device owners plan to try—without this page being a how-to.
- [AI 辅助剪藏与媒体笔记](./topics/ai-assisted-clipping-and-notes.md) — 面向播客、视频与笔记的浏览器剪藏/Obsidian 插件可较快积累用户，并把日常媒体消费与记笔记框成可用 AI 辅助的工作流。 / Browser clipping and Obsidian extensions for podcasts, video, and notes can gain users quickly and frame everyday media consumption and note-taking as AI-assisted workflows.
- [播客 AI 精细转写精读 / Podcast AI fine-transcription close reads](./topics/podcast-ai-fine-transcription-close-reads.md) — 播客 AI 阅读需区分精细转写与摘要的信息保留目标，并分别核验识别、说话人区分和术语准确性；多功能模型的能力转述不能替代原音频核验。 / AI podcast reading requires distinguishing the retention goals of fine transcripts and summaries and checking recognition, diarization, and terminology separately; reported multifunction model capabilities do not replace original-audio verification.
- [AI 媒体内容回灌训练语料](./topics/ai-media-training-corpus-loop.md) — 媒体可用 AI 写稿并由人播出；播出文本与「AI 味」表达再进入训练语料，形成生成→播出→再训练的大循环。 / Media can use AI-written scripts delivered by human hosts; broadcast text and AI-flavored phrasing then re-enter training corpora, forming a generate→broadcast→retrain loop.
- [中国特供版与本地应用预装](./topics/china-special-edition-preinstalls.md) — 面向中国市场的软件/系统「特供版」会预装淘宝、支付宝、爱奇艺、华为全家桶等本地主流应用，常以调侃口吻展示「塞满」观感。 / China-market “special editions” of software or systems preinstall local mainstream apps such as Taobao, Alipay, iQiyi, and the Huawei suite, often shown with a joking “stuffed full” framing.
- [设计品味驱动的 Linux 桌面试用](./topics/design-taste-led-linux-desktop-trial.md) — 新 Linux 桌面可在个人测试后公开试用；推荐信号可以是 demo/网站相对既有对照（如 Omarchy）的设计品味，试用面常落在闲置 PC，而非完整迁移主机器。 / A new Linux desktop can open for public trial after private testing; the recommend signal can be demo/site design taste versus a peer foil (e.g. Omarchy), with spare PCs as the usual try surface rather than full primary-machine migration.
- [设计↔代码与自带 Claude/Codex 订阅](./topics/design-to-code-byo-subscriptions.md) — 设计工具可把设计与代码做成往返闭环（连本地代码、屏幕变图层、画布直发），并宣传可用用户已有的 Claude / Codex 订阅；旁观者亦会以 Paper 一类同行为品味对照。 / Design tools can close a design↔code loop (connect local code, screens as layers, ship from canvas) and pitch using the user’s existing Claude / Codex subscription; onlookers may also foil peers such as Paper on taste.
- [应用内模型合作伙伴](./topics/in-app-model-partnerships.md) — Agent/AI 工具可把模型厂商收成软件内的正式合作伙伴，而不只是通用 API 接入；Magpie 以 Kimi 为首个合作伙伴。 / Agent/AI tools can take model providers as formal in-app partners, not only generic API plugs; Magpie names Kimi as its first partner.
- [Agent 经家居平台直控设备](./topics/agent-home-platform-control.md) — 家电接入家居平台后可由个人 Agent 直接操作；进一步按网上菜谱补足烤箱预置程序是已提出但尚待试做验证的用途。 / Personal agents can directly operate appliances connected to home platforms; extending oven presets with online recipes is a proposed use still awaiting a cooking trial.
- [技术滥用关切的连续性](./topics/tech-misuse-concern-continuity.md) — 对「本代人做出的技术被拿去伤害」的关切，可从学生时代反战、反对技术用于战争，延续到创办 AI 实验室后对 AI 被利用的日常担忧。 / Concern that technology one’s generation builds will be used for harm can run from student anti-war activism against tech used in war through to day-to-day worry about AI misuse after founding an AI lab.
- [个人 Agent 热潮与 Coding Agent 熟悉度差](./topics/personal-agent-hype-familiarity-gap.md) — 个人 Agent（如 Grok Bot、Muse）热潮对未高频用过前沿 Coding Agent 的人更像科幻震撼；天天用 Claude Code / Codex 的人波澜不大——熟悉度差决定体感；资本侧亦可见陡峭估值阶梯（如 Instinct 融资传闻）；手机端另有 session 便利差。 / Personal-agent crazes (e.g. Grok Bot, Muse) feel like sci-fi awe to people who rarely use frontier coding agents; daily Claude Code / Codex users barely flinch—the familiarity gap drives reception; capital markets also show steep valuation ladders (e.g. Instinct fundraise rumors); mobile also differs in session convenience.
- [个人 Agent 云环境的备份、隔离与定时检查](./topics/personal-agent-cloud-env-pitfalls.md) — 把带云电脑的个人 Agent 当长期助理时，勿当传统 VPS：宜外置持久化备份、按项目隔离共享机，并认清闲置休眠会停 cron、重建后软件不一定留、内存可气球回收；定时优先产品自带任务，并对重要例程做独立核对与补偿。 / When a personal agent with a cloud PC is a long-term assistant, do not treat it as a traditional VPS—prefer external persistence backups and per-project isolation on a shared host, and expect idle sleep to pause cron, software not to survive rebuilds, and memory balloon reclaim; prefer product built-in tasks for schedules, plus independent checks and compensation for important routines.
- [托管优于自建代理节点](./topics/managed-over-selfhosted-proxy-nodes.md) — 个人代理与模型落地、应用托管上，一线可判断自建 VPS 节点 ROI 与稳定性已不敌托管：日常用机场、Claude 用廉价家宽、静态 Pages、动态廉价云主机——自建仍可玩，但性价比与省心常输。 / For personal proxies, model landing, and app hosting, frontline takes can judge self-hosted VPS nodes as losing on ROI and stability to managed stacks—daily managed proxies, cheap residential for Claude landing, Pages for static, cheap cloud VMs for dynamic—self-host remains playable but often loses on value and ease.
- [个人 Agent 对外分享与 Bot 互聊](./topics/personal-agent-share-and-interbot-chat.md) — 个人 Agent 可经网关注册专有 ID 与网页，对外分享给亲友；Bot 之间可聊天、传文件、协商日程，并以单向关注与互关好友区分私信路由与隐私边界；同作者亦在 X 订阅粉丝群用 Grok Bot 草拟回复但逐条批准后再发，与 Yobiru 互关免批准并置。 / A personal agent can register a dedicated ID and page on a gateway for sharing with friends; bots can chat, send files, and negotiate schedules, with one-way follow versus mutual friends separating DM routing and privacy bounds; the same author also uses Grok Bot on X subscriber fan groups to draft replies but approves each before send—alongside, not canceling, Yobiru mutual-friend no-approval routing.
- [手机端 Agent 全链路开发](./topics/mobile-agent-dev-full-chain.md) — 个人 Agent 可从手机发起云端开发，经 Cursor Cloud、GitHub PR/CI 发布，也可直接部署 Cloudflare Workers；GitHub 在简单建站路径中可选，快速上线个案不替代代码、测试与权限核验。 / Personal agents can initiate cloud development from a phone, release through Cursor Cloud and GitHub PR/CI, or deploy directly to Cloudflare Workers; GitHub is optional for simple sites, and fast-launch accounts do not replace code, test, and permission checks.
- [手机连自有服务器操控 Agent 工作台](./topics/phone-own-server-agent-workbench.md) — 用手机客户端连自己的服务器，即可随时访问与操控桌面侧的多款编程 / harness Agent 与本地模型，并把多 Agent 协作、任务交接与已有订阅一并带上——把电脑上的 Agent 工作台装进手机。 / A phone client connected to your own server can remotely access and control desktop-side coding/harness agents and local models—carrying multi-agent collaboration, task handoff, and existing subscriptions along—putting the desktop agent workbench into the phone.
- [手机变轻与 Mac 专供 Agent](./topics/lighter-phone-macs-for-agents.md) — 个人硬件预算可从「年年换更重的手机」转向「Mac 等算力机专供 agent」；手机退成越来越轻的入口，重活与常驻算力落在给 agent 的机器上。 / Personal hardware spend can shift from yearly heavier phones to Macs (and peers) dedicated to agents; the phone shrinks into a lighter front door while heavy work and standing compute sit on agent-bound machines.
- [远程 AI Coding 时笔记本只需轻与续航](./topics/laptop-light-battery-remote-ai-coding.md) — 当外出把 AI Coding 连回家里（或他处）机器时，随身笔记本的核心需求可收成轻与续航，本地高性能机型（如旗舰 MacBook Pro）吸引力下降。 / When out-of-office AI coding runs by connecting back to a home (or other remote) machine, a travel laptop’s core needs can shrink to lightness and battery life, and flagship local performance (e.g. top MacBook Pro) loses appeal.
- [Agent 对话内问题反馈](./topics/agent-in-chat-bug-feedback.md) — 个人 Agent 可在对话中识别疑似 bug 后征求是否反馈，并代发日志与问题描述，降低用户写不清或不愿写反馈的摩擦。 / A personal agent can, after a suspected bug surfaces in chat, ask consent to report and ship logs plus a description—cutting friction from unclear write-ups or skipped reports.
- [Agent 产出直送 Notion 阅读](./topics/agent-output-to-notion-reading.md) — 个人 Agent（如 Grok bot、Muse）可将生成内容直接发到 Notion；设备端（如 iPhone Duo）适配良好时，Notion 成为可读的阅读面。 / Personal agents (e.g. Grok bot, Muse) can send generated content straight into Notion; when device adaptation (e.g. iPhone Duo) is strong, Notion becomes a viable reading surface.
- [阅读应用适配 iPhone Duo](./topics/reading-app-iphone-duo-adaptation.md) — 面向阅读/预览的应用会跟进 iPhone Duo 适配；配套能力还可包括记住上次阅读位置，使双屏机上的长文阅读可续读。 / Reading and preview apps follow up with iPhone Duo adaptation; companion features can include remembering the last reading position so long-form reading on dual-screen devices can resume.
- [AI 应用适配 iPhone Duo / AI apps adapting to iPhone Duo](./topics/ai-app-iphone-duo-adaptation.md) — WorkBuddy 与 Grok bot 均有适配 iPhone Duo 的社区报告，其中仅 WorkBuddy 的报告明确提到隔天跟进。 / Community reports describe iPhone Duo adaptation for WorkBuddy and Grok bot, with only the WorkBuddy report explicitly stating next-day follow-up.
- [iPhone Duo 普通人吐槽与开发者赞美](./topics/iphone-duo-user-vs-developer-reception.md) — 对新双屏机型（如 iPhone Duo）的公开反应可按人群分裂：普通人多吐槽缺点，iOS 开发者则一边倒赞美。 / Public reaction to a new dual-screen device (e.g. iPhone Duo) can split by audience—ordinary people complain about shortcomings while iOS developers praise it one-sidedly.
- [menubar 管理工具真空](./topics/menubar-management-tool-gap.md) — 即便通用 AI 能力很强，Mac menubar 图标管理一类日常工具仍可能长期缺「完美好用」选项——现有产品或难用、或更新不停仍不满意、或已停更。 / Even when general AI is strong, everyday Mac menubar icon managers can still lack a “polished and usable” option—current products may be hard to use, update often yet still disappoint, or sit abandoned.
- [纯语音全双工与情绪可听](./topics/pure-speech-full-duplex.md) — 仅从人类语音训练的 speech-to-speech 全双工模型，可保留语气与情绪线索，避免「语音→文本→LLM」压掉非文本信息；并被框成能力上升时「听不见痛苦还继续聊」的安全补丁。 / Speech-to-speech full-duplex models trained only on human voice can keep tone and emotion cues that voice→text→LLM stacks crush; they are also framed as a safety step against chatting on while deaf to pain as capability rises.

</details>
